! Likely an erased or reserved flash area.

! 000: ff ff ff ff

! ------------------------------------------------------------
! 0x1FC: Possible firmware marker
! ------------------------------------------------------------
!
! 32-bit big-endian value: 0xDEAD0008
! Located at the end of the first 512-byte block.

! Possible purposes:
!   - Firmware identification
!   - Bootloader validation marker
!   - Flash integrity sentinel
!
! No verification routine has been identified yet.
! The disassembler interprets this word as an STBA
! instruction, but it is more likely to be data.

1fc: de ad 00 08    ! .word 0xDEAD0008

!

! ------------------------------------------------------------
! 0x260 - 0x26F: Register window underflow trap
! ------------------------------------------------------------
!

! ------------------------------------------------------------
! 0x270 - 0x2A0: Low-level hardware output routine
! ------------------------------------------------------------
!
! Likely a polling-based serial output routine.
!
! MMIO status register: 0x20000064
! MMIO data register:   0x20000060
!
! Wait until status bit 2 is set.
! Write the value in %l6 to the output register.
! Execute a short delay loop and return.
!
! Note:
! This routine overlaps positions normally reserved
! for additional trap entries. Its exact entry path
! and runtime memory mapping remain unverified.

270: 2f 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %l7

274: c2 05 e0 64    ld    [%l7 + 0x64], %g1
278: 80 88 60 04    btst  4, %g1
27c: 02 bf ff fe    be    0x274
280: 01 00 00 00    nop

284: 2f 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %l7
288: ec 25 e0 60    st    %l6, [%l7 + 0x60]

! Delay loop: 256 iterations.

28c: ae 10 21 00    mov   0x100, %l7
290: ae a5 e0 01    deccc %l7
294: 12 bf ff ff    bne   0x290
298: 01 00 00 00    nop

! Return to caller.

29c: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
2a0: 01 00 00 00    nop

5c8: 82 10 00 13    mov     %l3, %g1            ! Restore original g1
5cc: 81 c4 40 00    jmp     %l1                 ! Jump to address in l1
5d0: 81 cc 80 00    rett    %l2  ! Trap return in jump delay slot

! 0x5D4–0x5F0: Rotate WIM and move to saved register window
5d4: a7 2c 20 01    sll     %l0, 1, %l3          ! l3 = l0 << 1

5f0: 81 e8 00 00    restore                       ! Move to previous window again

! 0x5F4–0x610: Reload register window from stack
5f4: e0 1b a0 00    ldd     [%sp], %l0           ! Restore l0 and l1
5f8: e4 1b a0 08    ldd     [%sp + 8], %l2       ! Restore l2 and l3
5fc: e8 1b a0 10    ldd     [%sp + 0x10], %l4    ! Restore l4 and l5
600: ec 1b a0 18    ldd     [%sp + 0x18], %l6    ! Restore l6 and l7

610: fc 1b a0 38    ldd     [%sp + 0x38], %fp    ! Restore i6 and i7

! 0x614–0x620: Restore register window and return from trap

618: 81 e0 00 00    save                        ! Move to next register window again
61c: 81 c4 40 00    jmp     %l1                 ! Restore saved PC
620: 81 cc 80 00    rett    %l2  ! Trap return in jump delay slot

! 0x624–0x648: Signed division helper

628: 81 82 a0 00    wr      %o2, %y              ! Y = sign extension of dividend
62c: 01 00 00 00    nop                           ! Y write latency

63c: 2e 80 00 02    bvs,a   0x644                ! Branch if overflow; annul delay slot if not taken

! 0x64C–0x65C: Unsigned division helper

654: 01 00 00 00    nop

! 0x660–0x674: Signed multiplication helper (entry likely 0x66C)

664: 01 00 00 00    nop  ! Padding or scheduling NOP (purpose unconfirmed)
668: 01 00 00 00    nop  ! Padding or scheduling NOP (purpose unconfirmed)

680: 01 00 00 00    nop  ! Padding or scheduling NOP (purpose unconfirmed)

! 0x690–0x6BC: Signed remainder helper (entry likely 0x694)

694: 99 3a 20 1f    sra     %o0, 0x1f, %o4      ! Sign extension

6e4: c6 0a 40 00    ldub  [%o1], %g3          ! g3 = unsigned byte from string 2
6e8: d4 0a 00 00    ldub  [%o0], %o2          ! o2 = unsigned byte from string 1

! 0x71C–0x72C: Check ASCII decimal digits for hexadecimal classification

768: 80 a2 20 0a    cmp   %o0, 0xa           ! Unsigned comparison with 10

770: 90 40 20 00    addx  %g0, 0, %o0  ! Return delay slot: convert carry flag to 0 or 1

! 0x774–0x780: Start string-to-integer conversion; check base argument
774: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp       ! Allocate stack frame
778: 80 a6 a0 00    cmp   %i2, 0              ! Check third argument

! 0x784–0x790: Check leading ASCII zero

! 0x794–0x7A4: Check optional hexadecimal prefix

7b0: 80 a2 20 00    cmp   %o0, 0              ! Check validator result
7b4: 02 80 00 16    be    0x80c              ! Skip if not hexadecimal

! 0x7BC–0x80B: Select numeric base and accumulate digits
! Steps:
! 1. Complete hexadecimal prefix detection.
! 2. Select multiplication by 8, 10, or 16.
! 3. Add the converted digit to the accumulated value.
! 4. Advance the input pointer.

! 0x7BC–0x7C4: Accept hexadecimal prefix
7bc: b0 06 20 01    inc  %i0                 ! Advance past 'x'
7c0: 10 80 00 13    b    0x80c              ! Continue parsing
7c4: b4 10 20 10    mov  0x10, %i2          ! Delay slot: base = 16

! 0x7C8–0x7FC: Select multiplication by base
7c8: 02 80 00 0d    be   0x7fc              ! Base 10: calculate value * 10

7d0: 18 80 00 06    bgu  0x7e8              ! Base > 10: check for base 16
7d4: 80 a6 a0 08    cmp  %i2, 8            ! Delay slot: compare base with 8

7e0: 10 80 00 0b    b    0x80c              ! Continue parsing
7e4: b0 06 20 01    inc  %i0                ! Delay slot: advance pointer

! 0x7E8–0x7FC: Handle base 16 or unsupported base
7e8: 80 a6 a0 10    cmp  %i2, 0x10         ! Check for base 16
7ec: 02 80 00 06    be   0x804             ! Base 16: use value * 16

7f4: 10 80 00 06    b    0x80c             ! Continue parsing
7f8: b0 06 20 01    inc  %i0                ! Delay slot: advance pointer

! 0x7FC–0x80B: Accumulate digit

800: 90 02 00 09    add  %o0, %o1, %o0     ! o0 = value * 10
804: a0 02 00 0a    add  %o0, %o2, %l0     ! Accumulator = product + digit
808: b0 06 20 01    inc  %i0                ! Advance input pointer    

! 0x80C–0x85B: Read and convert the next ASCII digit
! Steps:
! 1. Check whether the character is hexadecimal.
! 2. Distinguish decimal digits from alphabetic digits.
! 3. Convert ASCII to a numeric value.
! 4. Compare the digit with the selected base.
! 5. Repeat while the digit is valid.

! 0x80C–0x81C: Validate input character
80c: 7f ff ff c4    call  0x71c             ! Check hexadecimal character

814: 80 a2 20 00    cmp   %o0, 0          ! Check validator result

! 0x840–0x84C: Convert hexadecimal letter
840: 90 0a 3f df    and  %o0, -33, %o0     ! Clear ASCII lowercase bit
844: 91 2a 20 18    sll  %o0, 0x18, %o0   ! Shift left 24 bits
848: 91 3a 20 18    sra  %o0, 0x18, %o0   ! Sign-extend low byte
84c: 94 02 3f c9    add  %o0, -55, %o2    ! 'A' -> 10, ..., 'F' -> 15

! 0x850–0x85B: Check digit against base
850: 80 a2 80 1a    cmp  %o2, %i2         ! Compare digit with base
854: 0a bf ff dd    bcs  0x7c8            ! Continue if digit < base
858: 80 a6 a0 0a    cmp  %i2, 0xa         ! Delay slot: compare base with 10
85c: 80 a6 60 00    cmp  %i1, 0          ! Check end-pointer argument
! 0x85C–0x86C: Store end pointer and return parsed value
! Steps:
! 1. Check whether an end-pointer address was supplied.
! 2. Store the current input position when requested.
! 3. Restore the caller's register window.
! 4. Return the accumulated integer.

85c: 80 a6 60 00    cmp      %i1, 0            ! Is endptr NULL?

868: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                        ! Return
86c: 91 e8 00 10    restore  %g0, %l0, %o0    ! Delay slot: return accumulator

! 0x870–0x8A8: Signed string-to-integer conversion
! Steps:
! 1. Create a stack frame.
! 2. Read the first character and check for '-'.
! 3. Forward the end-pointer and base arguments.
! 4. Call the unsigned conversion routine at 0x774.
! 5. Negate the result if the input starts with '-'.
! 6. Return the converted integer.

! 0x870–0x884: Initialize arguments and check sign
870: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp      ! Create a 104-byte stack frame
874: d0 4e 00 00    ldsb  [%i0], %o0         ! Read first input character
878: 92 10 00 19    mov   %i1, %o1           ! Forward end-pointer argument
87c: 80 a2 20 2d    cmp   %o0, 0x2d         ! Compare with ASCII '-'
880: 02 80 00 06    be    0x898             ! Branch if negative
884: 94 10 00 1a    mov   %i2, %o2           ! Delay slot: forward base argument

! 0x888–0x894: Convert nonnegative input
888: 7f ff ff bb    call  0x774             ! Call unsigned conversion routine

890: 10 80 00 05    b     0x8a4            ! Skip negative conversion

! 0x898–0x8A0: Convert negative input
898: 7f ff ff b7    call  0x774             ! Convert digits after '-'
89c: 90 06 20 01    add   %i0, 1, %o0      ! Delay slot: skip minus sign
8a0: b0 20 00 08    neg   %o0, %i0         ! Negate converted value

! 0x8A4–0x8A8: Return result
8a4: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                     ! Return to caller
8a8: 81 e8 00 00    restore                 ! Delay slot: restore register window

! 0x8AC–0x8D4: Output a null-terminated string
! Steps:
! 1. Create a stack frame.
! 2. Load the first character.
! 3. Stop when the null terminator is reached.
! 4. Call the character-output routine at 0x998.
! 5. Advance the pointer and repeat.
! 6. Restore the register window and return.

! 0x8AC–0x8B4: Initialize string traversal
8ac: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp    ! Create stack frame
8b0: 10 80 00 05    b     0x8c4             ! Jump to initial character test
8b4: d0 0e 00 00    ldub  [%i0], %o0       ! Delay slot: load first character

! 0x8C4–0x8CC: Check for string termination
8c4: 80 a2 20 00    cmp   %o0, 0           ! Is current character NUL?

! 0x8D0–0x8D4: Return
8d0: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                     ! Return to caller
8d4: 81 e8 00 00    restore                 ! Delay slot: restore register window

! 0x8D8–0x900: Output a string and append a newline
! Steps:
! 1. Create a stack frame.
! 2. Read the first byte of the input string.
! 3. If the first byte is ASCII 8, print the remaining string.
! 4. Output ASCII 10 (line feed).
! 5. Restore the register window and return.

! 0x8D8–0x8E8: Check the first character
8d8: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp    ! Create stack frame
8dc: d0 0e 00 00    ldub  [%i0], %o0       ! Read first byte

8e4: 12 80 00 04    bne   0x8f4            ! Skip string output if not BS

8ec: 7f ff ff f0    call  0x8ac             ! Output string starting at input + 1
8f0: 90 06 20 01    add   %i0, 1, %o0      ! Delay slot: skip first byte

! 0x8F4–0x900: Output newline and return
8f4: 40 00 00 29    call  0x998             ! Call character-output routine
8f8: 90 10 20 0a    mov   0xa, %o0         ! Delay slot: ASCII LF (newline)
8fc: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                     ! Return to caller
900: 81 e8 00 00    restore                 ! Delay slot: restore register window

! 0x904–0x930: Output one hexadecimal digit
! Steps:
! 1. Extract the lowest four bits (one nibble).
! 2. Convert values 0–9 to ASCII '0'–'9'.
! 3. Convert values 10–15 to ASCII 'a'–'f'.
! 4. Send the resulting character to 0x998.
! 5. Return to the caller.

904: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp      ! Create stack frame
908: 90 0e 20 0f    and   %i0, 0xf, %o0      ! Extract low nibble
90c: 80 a2 20 09    cmp   %o0, 9            ! Compare nibble with 9
910: 18 80 00 05    bgu   0x924             ! Branch if value > 9
914: 01 00 00 00    nop                      ! Delay slot

918: 40 00 00 20    call  0x998             ! Output decimal hex digit
91c: 90 12 20 30    or    %o0, 0x30, %o0   ! Delay slot: convert to '0'–'9'
920: 30 80 00 03    b,a   0x92c             ! Unconditional branch, annul slot

924: 40 00 00 1d    call  0x998             ! Output alphabetic hex digit
928: 90 02 20 57    add   %o0, 0x57, %o0   ! Delay slot: convert to 'a'–'f'

92c: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                      ! Return
930: 81 e8 00 00    restore                  ! Delay slot

! 0x934–0x94C: Output an 8-bit value as two hexadecimal digits
! Steps:
! 1. Shift the input right by four bits.
! 2. Output the high nibble.
! 3. Output the low nibble.
! 4. Return.

934: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
938: 7f ff ff f3    call  0x904             ! Output high nibble
93c: 91 36 20 04    srl   %i0, 4, %o0     ! Delay slot: input >> 4
940: 7f ff ff f1    call  0x904             ! Output low nibble

948: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
94c: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0x950–0x970: Output a 16-bit value as four hexadecimal digits
! Steps:
! 1. Start at bit position 12.
! 2. Extract and output one nibble.
! 3. Decrease shift by four bits.
! 4. Repeat through bit position zero.
! 5. Return.

950: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
954: a0 10 20 0c    mov   0xc, %l0          ! Shift = 12

958: 7f ff ff eb    call  0x904             ! Output nibble
95c: 91 36 00 10    srl   %i0, %l0, %o0   ! Delay slot: extract nibble
960: a0 84 3f fc    addcc %l0, -4, %l0     ! Shift -= 4
964: 1c bf ff fd    bpos  0x958            ! Repeat while shift >= 0
968: 01 00 00 00    nop

96c: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
970: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0x974–0x994: Output a 32-bit value as eight hexadecimal digits
! Steps:
! 1. Start at bit position 28.
! 2. Extract and output each nibble from most to least significant.
! 3. Decrease shift by four bits.
! 4. Stop after all eight digits.
! 5. Return.

974: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
978: a0 10 20 1c    mov   0x1c, %l0         ! Shift = 28

97c: 7f ff ff e2    call  0x904             ! Output nibble
980: 91 36 00 10    srl   %i0, %l0, %o0   ! Delay slot: extract nibble
984: a0 84 3f fc    addcc %l0, -4, %l0     ! Shift -= 4
988: 1c bf ff fd    bpos  0x97c            ! Repeat while shift >= 0
98c: 01 00 00 00    nop

990: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
994: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0x998–0x9F0: Polled character output with LF-to-CRLF conversion
! Steps:
! 1. Extract the lowest eight bits of the character.
! 2. Check whether the character is LF (0x0A).
! 3. If LF, wait for transmitter readiness and output CR (0x0D).
! 4. Wait for transmitter readiness again.
! 5. Write the original character to the data register.
! 6. Return.

! 0x998–0x9A4: Check for line feed
998: 84 0a 20 ff    and   %o0, 0xff, %g2
99c: 80 a0 a0 0a    cmp   %g2, 0xa
9a0: 12 80 00 0b    bne   0x9cc
9a4: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2

! 0x9A8–0x9C8: Output carriage return before line feed
9a8: 86 10 a0 64    or    %g2, 0x64, %g3       ! Status register address
9ac: c4 00 c0 00    ld    [%g3], %g2           ! Read status
9b0: 80 88 a0 04    btst  4, %g2              ! Test status bit 2
9b4: 02 bf ff fe    be    0x9ac               ! Wait until bit 2 is set
9b8: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2 ! Delay slot
9bc: 84 10 a0 60    or    %g2, 0x60, %g2       ! Data register address
9c0: 86 10 20 0d    mov   0xd, %g3             ! ASCII CR
9c4: c6 20 80 00    st    %g3, [%g2]           ! Write CR
9c8: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2

! 0x9CC–0x9E8: Output original character
9cc: 86 10 a0 64    or    %g2, 0x64, %g3       ! Status register address
9d0: 90 0a 20 ff    and   %o0, 0xff, %o0       ! Keep lowest byte
9d4: c4 00 c0 00    ld    [%g3], %g2           ! Read status
9d8: 80 88 a0 04    btst  4, %g2              ! Test status bit 2
9dc: 02 bf ff fe    be    0x9d4               ! Wait until ready
9e0: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2 ! Delay slot
9e4: 84 10 a0 60    or    %g2, 0x60, %g2       ! Data register address
9e8: d0 20 80 00    st    %o0, [%g2]           ! Write character

! 0x9EC–0x9F0: Return
9ec: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
9f0: 01 00 00 00    nop

! 0x9F4–0xA14: Erase one character on a terminal
! Steps:
! 1. Send backspace (0x08).
! 2. Send a space (0x20).
! 3. Send backspace again.
! 4. Return.

9f4: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
9f8: 7f ff ff e8    call  0x998
9fc: 90 10 20 08    mov   8, %o0
a00: 7f ff ff e6    call  0x998
a04: 90 10 20 20    mov   0x20, %o0
a08: 7f ff ff e4    call  0x998
a0c: 90 10 20 08    mov   8, %o0
a10: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
a14: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0xA18–0xA78: Poll serial-interface status bits
! Steps:
! 1. Initialize MMIO base and a counter limit.
! 2. Wait until status bit 2 is set.
! 3. Wait until status bit 7 is set.
! 4. Wait until status bit 1 is set.
! 5. Repeat until the counter exceeds 0x1387.
! 6. Return.

! 0xA18–0xA30: Initialize addresses and counter
a18: 07 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g3
a1c: 05 00 00 04    sethi %hi(0x1000), %g2
a20: 94 10 00 03    mov   %g3, %o2
a24: 96 10 a3 87    or    %g2, 0x387, %o3      ! Limit = 0x1387
a28: 86 10 e0 64    or    %g3, 0x64, %g3      ! Status address
a2c: 84 10 20 00    clr   %g2
a30: 92 00 a0 01    add   %g2, 1, %o1        ! Counter = 1

! 0xA34–0xA40: Wait for bit 2
a34: c4 00 c0 00    ld    [%g3], %g2
a38: 80 88 a0 04    btst  4, %g2
a3c: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xa34
a40: 90 12 a0 64    or    %o2, 0x64, %o0

! 0xA44–0xA50: Wait for bit 7
a44: c4 02 00 00    ld    [%o0], %g2
a48: 80 88 a0 80    btst  0x80, %g2
a4c: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xa44
a50: 01 00 00 00    nop

! 0xA54–0xA64: Wait for bit 1
a54: 90 12 a0 64    or    %o2, 0x64, %o0
a58: c4 02 00 00    ld    [%o0], %g2
a5c: 80 88 a0 02    btst  2, %g2
a60: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xa58
a64: 84 10 00 09    mov   %o1, %g2

! 0xA68–0xA78: Check repetition count
a68: 80 a0 80 0b    cmp   %g2, %o3
a6c: 04 bf ff f2    ble   0xa34
a70: 92 00 a0 01    add   %g2, 1, %o1
a74: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
a78: 01 00 00 00    nop

! 0xA7C–0xA94: Initialize argument parsing
! Steps:
! 1. Clear the argument counter.
! 2. Check whether the maximum argument index is exceeded.
! 3. Load the current input character.
! 4. Remember the current input position.

a7c: 86 10 20 00    clr   %g3              ! Argument count = 0
a80: 80 a0 c0 0a    cmp   %g3, %o2         ! Compare count with limit
a84: 34 80 00 27    bg,a  0xb20            ! Return -1 if count > limit
a88: 90 10 3f ff    mov   -1, %o0          ! Annulled delay slot if not taken
a8c: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2       ! Read current character
a90: 10 80 00 03    b     0xa9c            ! Enter whitespace check
a94: 96 10 00 08    mov   %o0, %o3         ! Save current pointer

! 0xA98–0xAB8: Skip spaces and horizontal tabs
! Steps:
! 1. Read the current character.
! 2. Stop on NUL.
! 3. Advance past ASCII space or tab.
! 4. Stop at the first non-whitespace character.

a98: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2
a9c: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
aa0: 02 80 00 07    be    0xabc
aa4: 80 a0 a0 20    cmp   %g2, 0x20
aa8: 22 bf ff fc    be,a  0xa98
aac: 90 02 20 01    inc   %o0
ab0: 80 a0 a0 09    cmp   %g2, 9
ab4: 22 bf ff f9    be,a  0xa98
ab8: 90 02 20 01    inc   %o0

! 0xABC–0xAD8: Terminate previous token and check for end of input
! Steps:
! 1. Check whether a previous token exists.
! 2. Replace its separator with NUL.
! 3. Check whether the current character is NUL.
! 4. Return the count when no more tokens remain.

abc: 80 a0 e0 00    cmp   %g3, 0
ac0: 02 80 00 03    be    0xacc
ac4: c4 0a 00 00    ldub  [%o0], %g2
ac8: c0 2a c0 00    clrb  [%o3]
acc: 85 28 a0 18    sll   %g2, 0x18, %g2
ad0: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
ad4: 02 80 00 12    be    0xb1c
ad8: 85 28 e0 02    sll   %g3, 2, %g2

! 0xADC–0xAE8: Store the next argument pointer
! Steps:
! 1. Calculate argument index multiplied by four.
! 2. Store the current string pointer.
! 3. Increment argument count.

adc: d0 22 40 02    st    %o0, [%o1 + %g2] ! argv[argc] = p
ae0: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2       ! Read first token character
ae4: 10 80 00 03    b     0xaf0
ae8: 86 00 e0 01    inc   %g3              ! argc++

! 0xAEC–0xB08: Scan to the end of the current token
! Steps:
! 1. Read the next character.
! 2. Stop on NUL, space, or tab.
! 3. Otherwise advance and repeat.

aec: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2
af0: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
af4: 02 80 00 06    be    0xb0c
af8: 80 a0 a0 20    cmp   %g2, 0x20
afc: 02 80 00 04    be    0xb0c
b00: 80 a0 a0 09    cmp   %g2, 9
b04: 32 bf ff fa    bne,a  0xaec
b08: 90 02 20 01    inc   %o0


! 0xB0C–0xB24: Continue parsing or return argument count
! Steps:
! 1. Check whether the token ended at NUL.
! 2. If not, repeat the outer loop.
! 3. Otherwise return the number of arguments.

b0c: c4 4a 00 00    ldsb  [%o0], %g2
b10: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
b14: 12 bf ff dc    bne   0xa84
b18: 80 a0 c0 0a    cmp   %g3, %o2         ! Delay slot: compare count with limit
b1c: 90 10 00 03    mov   %g3, %o0         ! Return argument count
b20: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
b24: 01 00 00 00    nop

! 0xB28–0xBC0: Read an interactive command line
! Steps:
! 1. Initialize the input length to zero.
! 2. Read characters from the serial input routine at 0xBC4.
! 3. Echo ordinary characters and append them to the buffer.
! 4. Handle backspace (0x08), including BEL at an empty line.
! 5. Stop on carriage return (0x0D) or when the buffer is full.
! 6. NUL-terminate the input buffer.
!
! Arguments:
! %i0 = destination buffer
! %i1 = buffer capacity
! %l0 = current input length

b28: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
b2c: 10 80 00 09    b     0xb50
b30: a0 10 20 00    clr   %l0
b34: 90 0a 60 ff    and   %o1, 0xff, %o0
b38: 7f ff ff 98    call  0x998
b3c: a0 04 20 01    inc   %l0
b40: 90 06 7f ff    add   %i1, -1, %o0
b44: 80 a4 00 08    cmp   %l0, %o0
b48: 36 80 00 1d    bge,a 0xbbc
b4c: c0 2e 00 10    clrb  [%i0 + %l0]
b50: 40 00 00 1d    call  0xbc4
b54: 01 00 00 00    nop
b58: 92 10 00 08    mov   %o0, %o1
b5c: 91 2a 60 18    sll   %o1, 0x18, %o0
b60: 91 3a 20 18    sra   %o0, 0x18, %o0
b64: 80 a2 20 08    cmp   %o0, 8
b68: 12 80 00 0e    bne   0xba0
b6c: 80 a2 20 0d    cmp   %o0, 0xd
b70: 80 a4 20 00    cmp   %l0, 0
b74: 12 80 00 06    bne   0xb8c
b78: 90 10 20 20    mov   0x20, %o0
b7c: 7f ff ff 87    call  0x998
b80: 90 10 20 07    mov   7, %o0
b84: 10 bf ff f0    b     0xb44
b88: 90 06 7f ff    add   %i1, -1, %o0
b8c: d0 2e 00 10    stb   %o0, [%i0 + %l0]
b90: 7f ff ff 99    call  0x9f4
b94: a0 04 3f ff    add   %l0, -1, %l0
b98: 10 bf ff eb    b     0xb44
b9c: 90 06 7f ff    add   %i1, -1, %o0
ba0: 32 bf ff e5    bne,a 0xb34
ba4: d2 2e 00 10    stb   %o1, [%i0 + %l0]
ba8: 7f ff ff 7c    call  0x998
bac: 90 0a 60 ff    and   %o1, 0xff, %o0
bb0: 7f ff ff 7a    call  0x998
bb4: 90 10 20 0a    mov   0xa, %o0
bb8: c0 2e 00 10    clrb  [%i0 + %l0]
bbc: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
bc0: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0xBC4–0xBF8: Read one character from the serial interface
! Steps:
! 1. Execute a short delay loop.
! 2. Set the status register address to 0x20000064.
! 3. Poll status bit 0 until received data is available.
! 4. Read the data register at 0x20000060.
! 5. Return the received byte in %o0.
!
! Hardware:
! 0x20000064 = probable UART status register
! 0x20000060 = probable UART data register

bc4: 84 10 20 63    mov   0x63, %g2
bc8: 84 80 bf ff    addcc %g2, -1, %g2
bcc: 1c 80 00 00    bpos  0xbcc
bd0: 84 80 bf ff    addcc %g2, -1, %g2
bd4: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2
bd8: 86 10 a0 64    or    %g2, 0x64, %g3
bdc: c4 00 c0 00    ld    [%g3], %g2
be0: 80 88 a0 01    btst  1, %g2
be4: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xbdc
be8: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2
bec: 84 10 a0 60    or    %g2, 0x60, %g2
bf0: d0 00 80 00    ld    [%g2], %o0
bf4: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
bf8: 90 0a 20 ff    and   %o0, 0xff, %o0

! 0xBFC–0xC94: Poll serial input for 'i' or 'I'
! Steps:
! 1. Execute a short initial delay.
! 2. Initialize the UART status and data register addresses.
! 3. Poll the receive-ready bit with a timeout counter.
! 4. Read a character and compare it with 'i' (0x69) or 'I' (0x49).
! 5. Return 0 when either character is received.
! 6. Return 0xFF after a timeout or too many other characters.
!
! Hardware:
! 0x20000064 = probable UART status register
! 0x20000060 = probable UART data register
!
! Note:
! The timeout counter and character counter are independent.
! The character counter is checked through its low byte.

bfc: 86 10 20 63    mov   0x63, %g3
c00: 86 80 ff ff    addcc %g3, -1, %g3
c04: 1c 80 00 00    bpos  0xc04
c08: 86 80 ff ff    addcc %g3, -1, %g3
c0c: 05 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %g2
c10: 9a 10 00 02    mov   %g2, %o5
c14: 98 10 a0 64    or    %g2, 0x64, %o4
c18: 96 10 a0 60    or    %g2, 0x60, %o3
c1c: 86 10 20 00    clr   %g3
c20: 94 10 20 00    clr   %o2
c24: c4 03 00 00    ld    [%o4], %g2
c28: 80 88 a0 01    btst  1, %g2
c2c: 12 80 00 0c    bne   0xc5c
c30: 01 00 00 00    nop
c34: 13 00 01 40    sethi %hi(0x50000), %o1
c38: 90 13 60 64    or    %o5, 0x64, %o0
c3c: 86 00 e0 01    inc   %g3
c40: 80 a0 c0 09    cmp   %g3, %o1
c44: 34 80 00 13    bg,a  0xc90
c48: 90 10 20 ff    mov   0xff, %o0
c4c: c4 02 00 00    ld    [%o0], %g2
c50: 80 88 a0 01    btst  1, %g2
c54: 22 bf ff fb    be,a  0xc40
c58: 86 00 e0 01    inc   %g3
c5c: c4 02 c0 00    ld    [%o3], %g2
c60: 84 08 a0 ff    and   %g2, 0xff, %g2
c64: 80 a0 a0 69    cmp   %g2, 0x69
c68: 02 80 00 04    be    0xc78
c6c: 80 a0 a0 49    cmp   %g2, 0x49
c70: 12 80 00 04    bne   0xc80
c74: 94 02 a0 01    inc   %o2
c78: 10 80 00 06    b     0xc90
c7c: 90 10 20 00    clr   %o0
c80: 84 0a a0 ff    and   %o2, 0xff, %g2
c84: 80 a0 a0 02    cmp   %g2, 2
c88: 08 bf ff e7    bleu  0xc24
c8c: 90 10 20 ff    mov   0xff, %o0
c90: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
c94: 01 00 00 00    nop

! 0xC98–0xD38: Initialize and configure a hardware controller
! Steps:
! 1. Combine the two input arguments into a configuration word.
! 2. Write the configuration to register 0x2001041C.
! 3. Issue command 0x00280001 through register 0x20010418.
! 4. Wait for status bit 2 to become set.
! 5. Write 1 to the configuration register.
! 6. Issue command 0x00240001 and wait for completion.
! 7. Call the delay routine at 0x272C with argument 5.
! 8. Clear the configuration register.
! 9. Issue command 0x00240001 again and wait for completion.
!
! Arguments:
! %i0 = first configuration value
! %i1 = second configuration value
!
! Hardware:
! 0x20010418 = probable command/status register
! 0x2001041C = probable configuration/data register
!
! Note:
! Controller type and command meanings are not yet identified.

c98: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
c9c: 13 08 00 41    sethi %hi(0x20010400), %o1
ca0: b1 2e 20 08    sll   %i0, 8, %i0
ca4: 94 12 60 1c    or    %o1, 0x1c, %o2
ca8: b0 16 00 19    or    %i0, %i1, %i0
cac: 11 00 0a 00    sethi %hi(0x280000), %o0
cb0: f0 22 80 00    st    %i0, [%o2]
cb4: 92 12 60 18    or    %o1, 0x18, %o1
cb8: 90 12 20 01    or    %o0, 1, %o0
cbc: 94 10 00 09    mov   %o1, %o2
cc0: d0 22 40 00    st    %o0, [%o1]
cc4: d0 02 80 00    ld    [%o2], %o0
cc8: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0
ccc: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xcc4
cd0: 13 08 00 41    sethi %hi(0x20010400), %o1
cd4: 96 12 60 1c    or    %o1, 0x1c, %o3
cd8: 94 10 20 01    mov   1, %o2
cdc: 11 00 09 00    sethi %hi(0x240000), %o0
ce0: d4 22 c0 00    st    %o2, [%o3]
ce4: 92 12 60 18    or    %o1, 0x18, %o1
ce8: 90 12 20 01    or    %o0, 1, %o0
cec: 94 10 00 09    mov   %o1, %o2
cf0: d0 22 40 00    st    %o0, [%o1]
cf4: d0 02 80 00    ld    [%o2], %o0
cf8: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0
cfc: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xcf4
d00: 01 00 00 00    nop
d04: 40 00 06 8a    call  0x272c
d08: 90 10 20 05    mov   5, %o0
d0c: 11 08 00 41    sethi %hi(0x20010400), %o0
d10: 94 12 20 1c    or    %o0, 0x1c, %o2
d14: 13 00 09 00    sethi %hi(0x240000), %o1
d18: 90 12 20 18    or    %o0, 0x18, %o0
d1c: c0 22 80 00    clr   [%o2]
d20: 92 12 60 01    or    %o1, 1, %o1
d24: 94 10 00 08    mov   %o0, %o2
d28: d2 22 00 00    st    %o1, [%o0]
d2c: d0 02 80 00    ld    [%o2], %o0
d30: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0
d34: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xd2c
d38: 11 00 0d 00    sethi %hi(0x340000), %o0


! 0xD3C–0xDC4: Poll the hardware controller with retries
! Steps:
! 1. Prepare command words 0x00340003 and 0x00300003.
! 2. Set a retry counter to 1000.
! 3. Call the delay routine at 0x272C with argument 5.
! 4. Issue the first command and wait for status bit 2.
! 5. Read the data register and test bit 0.
! 6. If bit 0 is set, decrement the retry counter and repeat.
! 7. Otherwise issue the second command and read the response.
! 8. Mask the response with 0xFFFF and return it.
! 9. Return -1 if the retry counter expires.
!
! Hardware:
! 0x20010418 = probable command/status register
! 0x2001041C = probable data register

d3c: a6 12 20 03    or    %o0, 3, %l3
d40: 15 08 00 41    sethi %hi(0x20010400), %o2
d44: 13 00 0c 00    sethi %hi(0x300000), %o1
d48: 11 00 00 3f    sethi %hi(0xfc00), %o0
d4c: aa 10 00 0a    mov   %o2, %l5
d50: a4 12 60 03    or    %o1, 3, %l2
d54: a8 12 23 ff    or    %o0, 0x3ff, %l4
d58: b2 12 a0 18    or    %o2, 0x18, %i1
d5c: a2 12 a0 1c    or    %o2, 0x1c, %l1
d60: a0 10 23 e8    mov   0x3e8, %l0
d64: 40 00 06 72    call  0x272c
d68: 90 10 20 05    mov   5, %o0
d6c: e6 26 40 00    st    %l3, [%i1]
d70: d0 06 40 00    ld    [%i1], %o0
d74: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0
d78: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xd70
d7c: 01 00 00 00    nop
d80: f0 04 40 00    ld    [%l1], %i0
d84: 80 8e 20 01    btst  1, %i0
d88: 32 80 00 0b    bne,a 0xdb4
d8c: a0 04 3f ff    add   %l0, -1, %l0
d90: e4 26 40 00    st    %l2, [%i1]
d94: 94 15 60 18    or    %l5, 0x18, %o2
d98: d0 02 80 00    ld    [%o2], %o0
d9c: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0
da0: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xd98
da4: 01 00 00 00    nop
da8: f0 04 40 00    ld    [%l1], %i0
dac: 10 80 00 05    b     0xdc0
db0: b0 0e 00 14    and   %i0, %l4, %i0
db4: 80 a4 20 00    cmp   %l0, 0
db8: 14 bf ff eb    bg    0xd64
dbc: b0 10 3f ff    mov   -1, %i0
dc0: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
dc4: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0xDC8–0xE84: Write a value through the hardware controller
! Steps:
! 1. Combine the first two arguments into a configuration word.
! 2. Write the configuration to register 0x2001041C.
! 3. Issue command 0x00280001 and wait for completion.
! 4. Initialize a retry counter to 1000.
! 5. Issue command 0x00340003 and read the response.
! 6. Retry while response bit 0 is set.
! 7. Otherwise write the third argument to the data register.
! 8. Issue command 0x002C0001 and wait for completion.
! 9. Return 0 on success or -1 after exhausting retries.
!
! Arguments:
! %i0 = first configuration value
! %i1 = second configuration value
! %i2 = value to write
!
! Hardware:
! 0x20010418 = probable command/status register
! 0x2001041C = probable data register

dc8: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
dcc: 11 08 00 41    sethi %hi(0x20010400), %o0
dd0: b1 2e 20 08    sll   %i0, 8, %i0
dd4: 94 12 20 1c    or    %o0, 0x1c, %o2
dd8: b0 16 00 19    or    %i0, %i1, %i0
ddc: 13 00 0a 00    sethi %hi(0x280000), %o1
de0: f0 22 80 00    st    %i0, [%o2]
de4: 90 12 20 18    or    %o0, 0x18, %o0
de8: 92 12 60 01    or    %o1, 1, %o1
dec: 94 10 00 08    mov   %o0, %o2
df0: d2 22 00 00    st    %o1, [%o0]
df4: b0 10 3f ff    mov   -1, %i0
df8: d0 02 80 00    ld    [%o2], %o0
dfc: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0
e00: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xdf8
e04: 11 08 00 41    sethi %hi(0x20010400), %o0
e08: 13 00 0d 00    sethi %hi(0x340000), %o1
e0c: 15 00 0b 00    sethi %hi(0x2c0000), %o2
e10: a2 10 00 08    mov   %o0, %l1
e14: a4 12 60 03    or    %o1, 3, %l2
e18: a6 12 a0 01    or    %o2, 1, %l3
e1c: b2 12 20 18    or    %o0, 0x18, %i1
e20: a0 10 23 e8    mov   0x3e8, %l0
e24: 40 00 06 42    call  0x272c
e28: 90 10 20 05    mov   5, %o0
e2c: e4 26 40 00    st    %l2, [%i1]
e30: d0 06 40 00    ld    [%i1], %o0
e34: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0
e38: 02 bf ff fe    be    0xe30
e3c: 92 14 60 1c    or    %l1, 0x1c, %o1
e40: d0 02 40 00    ld    [%o1], %o0
e44: 80 8a 20 01    btst  1, %o0
e48: 32 80 00 0b    bne,a 0xe74
e4c: a0 04 3f ff    add   %l0, -1, %l0
e50: f4 22 40 00    st    %i2, [%o1]
e54: e6 26 40 00    st    %l3, [%i1]
e58: 92 14 60 18    or    %l1, 0x18, %o1
e5c: d0 02 40 00    ld    [%o1], %o0

! 0x1324–0x1498: Controller data transfer and status handling
! Steps:
! 1. Drain pending data while controller status bit 12 is set.
! 2. Enable or reset the controller if status bit 29 is clear.
! 3. Configure transfer registers using the first two arguments.
! 4. Poll for incoming data with a bounded retry counter.
! 5. Read two 32-bit words from the controller data port.
! 6. Check bit 23 of the second word.
! 7. Save diagnostic values to RAM when the check succeeds.
! 8. Return 0, -1 on timeout, or -3 on invalid data.
!
! Arguments:
! %i0 = transfer address or offset (exact meaning unknown)
! %i1 = transfer configuration value (exact meaning unknown)
! %i2 = destination buffer for two 32-bit words
!
! Hardware:
! 0x20010400 = controller status/control register
! 0x20010420 = controller data port
! 0x20002810 = auxiliary controller register
! 0x20002818 = transfer address register (probable)
! 0x2000281C = transfer configuration register (probable)

1344: d0 02 80 00    ld    [%o2], %o0          ! Read first data word

134c: d2 02 80 00    ld    [%o2], %o1          ! Read second data word

! 0x149C–0x18F8: Controller transfer, response validation and buffer preparation
! Steps:
! 1. Configure the controller and wait for transfer completion.
! 2. Read two result words and validate their status bits.
! 3. Wait for the auxiliary controller to become idle.
! 4. Validate selected fields against reference data in RAM.
! 5. Copy or construct a response buffer, optionally calling 0x1324.
! 6. Return a status code (0, 1, 2, 3, or negative error codes).

17e4: d4 0a 60 16    ldub  [ %o1 + 0x16 ], %o2         ! Read input byte at +0x16 + index
17e8: 96 02 e0 01    inc  %o3                          
17ec: 80 a2 e0 05    cmp  %o3, 5                       
17f0: 04 bf ff fa    ble  0x17d8                       
17f4: d4 2a 20 20    stb  %o2, [ %o0 + 0x20 ]          ! Store byte at destination + index + 0x20
17f8: 96 10 20 00    clr  %o3                          
17fc: 19 0c 00 0f    sethi  %hi(0x30003c00), %o4       
1800: d0 03 20 58    ld  [ %o4 + 0x58 ], %o0           
1804: 92 02 c0 18    add  %o3, %i0, %o1                
1808: 90 02 c0 08    add  %o3, %o0, %o0                
180c: d4 0a 60 1c    ldub  [ %o1 + 0x1c ], %o2         ! Read input byte at +0x1C + index
1810: 96 02 e0 01    inc  %o3                          
1814: 80 a2 e0 03    cmp  %o3, 3                       
1818: 04 bf ff fa    ble  0x1800                       
181c: d4 2a 20 26    stb  %o2, [ %o0 + 0x26 ]          ! Store byte at destination + index + 0x26
1820: d2 03 60 58    ld  [ %o5 + 0x58 ], %o1           ! Load destination pointer
1824: 90 10 20 02    mov  2, %o0                       ! Set byte value 2
1828: d0 2a 60 15    stb  %o0, [ %o1 + 0x15 ]          ! Write value 2 at destination + 0x15
182c: d0 03 60 58    ld  [ %o5 + 0x58 ], %o0           ! Load destination pointer as first argument
1830: 94 10 00 02    mov  %g2, %o2                     ! Pass local buffer at %fp - 16 as third argument
1834: 7f ff fe bc    call  0x1324                      ! Call previous controller transfer routine
1838: 92 10 20 2a    mov  0x2a, %o1                    ! Second argument: 42 (delay slot)
183c: 80 a2 20 00    cmp  %o0, 0                       ! Check transfer result
1840: 12 80 00 2d    bne  0x18f4                       ! Branch on nonzero result
1844: b0 10 3f fb    mov  -5, %i0                      ! Set return -5 (delay slot)
1848: 10 80 00 2b    b  0x18f4                         ! Return 2 on success
184c: b0 10 20 02    mov  2, %i0                       ! Set return 2 (delay slot)
1850: 10 80 00 29    b  0x18f4                         ! Auxiliary status timeout exit
1854: b0 10 3f fc    mov  -4, %i0                      ! Return -4 (delay slot)
1858: 90 0b 20 ff    and  %o4, 0xff, %o0               
185c: 80 a2 20 08    cmp  %o0, 8                       ! Check field at +0x0C against 8
1860: 32 80 00 25    bne,a   0x18f4                    ! Return 0 for other field values
1864: b0 10 20 00    clr  %i0                          
1868: 11 0c 00 0f    sethi  %hi(0x30003c00), %o0       ! Reference bytes at 0x30003C40
186c: d4 0a 20 40    ldub  [ %o0 + 0x40 ], %o2         
1870: d2 0e 20 1e    ldub  [ %i0 + 0x1e ], %o1         ! Read input byte at +0x1E
1874: 80 a2 40 0a    cmp  %o1, %o2                     ! Compare reference byte 0
1878: 12 80 00 1e    bne  0x18f0                       
187c: 96 12 20 40    or  %o0, 0x40, %o3                
1880: d2 0e 20 1f    ldub  [ %i0 + 0x1f ], %o1         ! Read input byte at +0x1F
1884: d0 0a e0 01    ldub  [ %o3 + 1 ], %o0            
1888: 80 a2 40 08    cmp  %o1, %o0                     
188c: 32 80 00 1a    bne,a   0x18f4                    
1890: b0 10 20 00    clr  %i0                          
1894: d2 0e 20 20    ldub  [ %i0 + 0x20 ], %o1         ! Read input byte at +0x20
1898: d0 0a e0 02    ldub  [ %o3 + 2 ], %o0            
189c: 80 a2 40 08    cmp  %o1, %o0                     
18a0: 32 80 00 15    bne,a   0x18f4                    
18a4: b0 10 20 00    clr  %i0                          
18a8: d2 0a e0 03    ldub  [ %o3 + 3 ], %o1            ! Read reference byte 3
18ac: d0 0e 20 21    ldub  [ %i0 + 0x21 ], %o0         ! Read input byte at +0x21
18b0: 80 a2 00 09    cmp  %o0, %o1                     
18b4: 32 80 00 10    bne,a   0x18f4                    
18b8: b0 10 20 00    clr  %i0                          
18bc: 11 0c 00 0e    sethi  %hi(0x30003800), %o0       ! RAM base 0x30003800
18c0: d4 02 23 48    ld  [ %o0 + 0x348 ], %o2          ! Read reference word at 0x30003B48
18c4: d2 0e 20 24    ldub  [ %i0 + 0x24 ], %o1         ! Read input byte at +0x24
18c8: 91 32 a0 08    srl  %o2, 8, %o0                  ! Extract bits 8–15
18cc: 90 0a 20 ff    and  %o0, 0xff, %o0               ! Mask to one byte
18d0: 80 a2 40 08    cmp  %o1, %o0                     ! Compare byte at +0x24
18d4: 32 80 00 08    bne,a   0x18f4                    
18d8: b0 10 20 00    clr  %i0                          
18dc: d2 0e 20 25    ldub  [ %i0 + 0x25 ], %o1         ! Read input byte at +0x25
18e0: 90 0a a0 ff    and  %o2, 0xff, %o0               ! Extract low byte
18e4: 80 a2 40 08    cmp  %o1, %o0                     ! Compare byte at +0x25
18e8: 02 80 00 03    be  0x18f4                        ! Return 3 when both bytes match
18ec: b0 10 20 03    mov  3, %i0                       ! Set return 3 (delay slot)
18f0: b0 10 20 00    clr  %i0                          ! Return 0
18f4: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                               ! Return to caller
18f8: 81 e8 00 00    restore                           ! Restore register window 
    
! 0x18FC–0x1964: Parse dot-separated decimal values
! Steps:
! 1. Read decimal digits from the input string.
! 2. Accumulate each value using multiplication by 10.
! 3. Store the accumulated byte on '.' or NUL.
! 4. Continue until the input string ends.
!
! Arguments:
! %o0 = destination byte array
! %o1 = NUL-terminated input string

18fc: 98 10 20 00    clr   %o4                  ! Destination index
1900: 96 10 20 00    clr   %o3                  ! Decimal accumulator
1904: c6 4a 40 00    ldsb  [%o1], %g3          ! Read current character
1908: 80 a0 e0 2e    cmp   %g3, 0x2e           ! ASCII '.'
190c: 02 80 00 06    be    0x1924              ! Store accumulated byte
1910: c4 0a 40 00    ldub  [%o1], %g2          ! Delay slot: unsigned character
1914: 84 00 bf d0    add   %g2, -48, %g2      ! Character minus ASCII '0'
1918: 80 a0 e0 00    cmp   %g3, 0              ! End of string?
191c: 12 80 00 06    bne   0x1934              ! Process non-NUL character
1920: 94 08 a0 ff    and   %g2, 0xff, %o2     ! Candidate digit (delay slot)
1924: d6 2a 00 0c    stb   %o3, [%o0 + %o4]   ! Store accumulated value
1928: 98 03 20 01    inc   %o4                  ! Next destination byte
192c: 10 80 00 09    b     0x1950
1930: 96 10 20 00    clr   %o3                  ! Reset accumulator
1934: 85 2a e0 03    sll   %o3, 3, %g2        ! Accumulator * 8
1938: 84 00 80 0b    add   %g2, %o3, %g2      ! Accumulator * 9
193c: 84 00 80 0b    add   %g2, %o3, %g2      ! Accumulator * 10
1940: 80 a2 a0 09    cmp   %o2, 9              ! Check digit range
1944: 18 80 00 03    bgu   0x1950              ! Ignore invalid character
1948: 84 00 bf d0    add   %g2, -48, %g2      ! Prepare decimal adjustment
194c: 96 00 80 03    add   %g2, %g3, %o3      ! Accumulator = old * 10 + digit
1950: c4 4a 40 00    ldsb  [%o1], %g2
1954: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
1958: 12 bf ff eb    bne   0x1904              ! Continue until NUL
195c: 92 02 60 01    inc   %o1                  ! Advance input pointer
1960: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
1964: 01 00 00 00    nop


! 0x1968–0x1A08: Parse colon-separated hexadecimal values
! Steps:
! 1. Read hexadecimal digits (0–9, a–f, A–F).
! 2. Shift the accumulator left by four bits per digit.
! 3. Store a byte on ':' or NUL.
! 4. Continue until the input string ends.
!
! Arguments:
! %o0 = destination byte array
! %o1 = NUL-terminated input string

1968: 88 10 20 00    clr   %g4                  ! Destination index
196c: 82 10 20 00    clr   %g1                  ! Hex accumulator
1970: d6 4a 40 00    ldsb  [%o1], %o3
1974: 80 a2 e0 3a    cmp   %o3, 0x3a           ! ASCII ':'
1978: 02 80 00 06    be    0x1990              ! Store accumulated byte
197c: d4 0a 40 00    ldub  [%o1], %o2
1980: 84 02 bf d0    add   %o2, -48, %g2      ! Candidate decimal digit
1984: 80 a2 e0 00    cmp   %o3, 0
1988: 12 80 00 06    bne   0x19a0
198c: 98 08 a0 ff    and   %g2, 0xff, %o4
1990: c2 2a 00 04    stb   %g1, [%o0 + %g4]   ! Store accumulated byte
1994: 88 01 20 01    inc   %g4                  ! Next output position
1998: 10 80 00 17    b     0x19f4
199c: 82 10 20 00    clr   %g1                  ! Reset accumulator
19a0: 85 28 60 04    sll   %g1, 4, %g2        ! Accumulator * 16
19a4: 86 02 bf 9f    add   %o2, -97, %g3      ! Candidate lowercase a–f
19a8: 84 00 bf d0    add   %g2, -48, %g2
19ac: 80 a3 20 09    cmp   %o4, 9
19b0: 18 80 00 04    bgu   0x19c0              ! Not a decimal digit
19b4: 9a 08 e0 ff    and   %g3, 0xff, %o5
19b8: 10 80 00 0f    b     0x19f4
19bc: 82 00 80 0b    add   %g2, %o3, %g1      ! Append decimal hex digit
19c0: 85 28 60 04    sll   %g1, 4, %g2
19c4: 86 02 bf bf    add   %o2, -65, %g3      ! Candidate uppercase A–F
19c8: 84 00 80 0b    add   %g2, %o3, %g2
19cc: 80 a3 60 05    cmp   %o5, 5
19d0: 18 80 00 04    bgu   0x19e0              ! Not lowercase a–f
19d4: 86 08 e0 ff    and   %g3, 0xff, %g3
19d8: 10 80 00 07    b     0x19f4
19dc: 82 00 bf a9    add   %g2, -87, %g1      ! Append lowercase hex digit
19e0: 85 28 60 04    sll   %g1, 4, %g2
19e4: 80 a0 e0 05    cmp   %g3, 5
19e8: 18 80 00 03    bgu   0x19f4              ! Ignore invalid character
19ec: 84 00 80 0b    add   %g2, %o3, %g2
19f0: 82 00 bf c9    add   %g2, -55, %g1      ! Append uppercase hex digit
19f4: c4 4a 40 00    ldsb  [%o1], %g2
19f8: 80 a0 a0 00    cmp   %g2, 0
19fc: 12 bf ff dd    bne   0x1970
1a00: 92 02 60 01    inc   %o1                  ! Advance input pointer
1a04: 81 c3 e0 08    retl
1a08: 01 00 00 00    nop


! 0x1A0C–0x1BAC: Prepare and execute a controller transfer sequence
! Steps:
! 1. Copy a 42-byte template into the active transfer buffer.
! 2. Populate selected fields from stored configuration values.
! 3. Clear a second 42-byte buffer.
! 4. Execute two initial transfers using routine 0x1324.
! 5. Poll for a matching response using routine 0x149C.
! 6. Retry up to the caller-supplied attempt limit.
!
! Arguments:
! %i0 = maximum number of outer attempts
!
! RAM:
! 0x30003B40 = source configuration bytes
! 0x30003B60 = 42-byte transfer template
! 0x30003C40 = four-byte stored identifier
! 0x30003C48 = four-byte stored configuration value
! 0x30003C58 = pointer to active transfer buffer
! 0x30003C5C = pointer to secondary buffer

1a0c: 9d e3 bf 88    save  %sp, -120, %sp
1a10: a8 10 20 00    clr   %l4                  ! Outer attempt counter
1a14: 80 a5 00 18    cmp   %l4, %i0
1a18: 36 80 00 64    bge,a 0x1ba8              ! Attempt limit reached
1a1c: b0 10 20 02    mov   2, %i0              ! Return 2 (annulled delay slot)
1a20: 11 0c 00 0e    sethi %hi(0x30003800), %o0
1a24: ac 12 23 60    or    %o0, 0x360, %l6    ! Template at 0x30003B60
1a28: 27 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %l3
1a2c: aa 07 bf f0    add   %fp, -16, %l5      ! Local result buffer

1a30: 94 10 20 00    clr   %o2                  ! Copy index
1a34: d2 0d 80 0a    ldub  [%l6 + %o2], %o1   ! Read template byte
1a38: d0 04 e0 58    ld    [%l3 + 0x58], %o0  ! Active buffer pointer
1a3c: d2 2a 00 0a    stb   %o1, [%o0 + %o2]   ! Copy template byte
1a40: 94 02 a0 01    inc   %o2
1a44: 80 a2 a0 2a    cmp   %o2, 0x2a           ! 42 bytes
1a48: 26 bf ff fc    bl,a 0x1a38
1a4c: d2 0d 80 0a    ldub  [%l6 + %o2], %o1

1a50: 11 0c 00 0e    sethi %hi(0x30003800), %o0
1a54: 98 12 23 40    or    %o0, 0x340, %o4   ! Source at 0x30003B40
1a58: 94 10 20 00    clr   %o2
1a5c: 17 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o3
1a60: d0 02 e0 58    ld    [%o3 + 0x58], %o0
1a64: d2 0b 00 0a    ldub  [%o4 + %o2], %o1
1a68: 90 02 80 08    add   %o2, %o0, %o0
1a6c: 94 02 a0 01    inc   %o2
1a70: 80 a2 a0 05    cmp   %o2, 5
1a74: 04 bf ff fb    ble   0x1a60
1a78: d2 2a 20 06    stb   %o1, [%o0 + 6]    ! Copy six bytes to offset 6

1a7c: 11 0c 00 0e    sethi %hi(0x30003800), %o0
1a80: 98 12 23 40    or    %o0, 0x340, %o4
1a84: 94 10 20 00    clr   %o2
1a88: 17 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o3
1a8c: d0 02 e0 58    ld    [%o3 + 0x58], %o0
1a90: d2 0b 00 0a    ldub  [%o4 + %o2], %o1
1a94: 90 02 80 08    add   %o2, %o0, %o0
1a98: 94 02 a0 01    inc   %o2
1a9c: 80 a2 a0 05    cmp   %o2, 5
1aa0: 04 bf ff fb    ble   0x1a8c
1aa4: d2 2a 20 16    stb   %o1, [%o0 + 0x16] ! Copy six bytes to offset 0x16

1aa8: 11 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o0
1aac: 98 12 20 40    or    %o0, 0x40, %o4   ! Identifier at 0x30003C40
1ab0: 94 10 20 00    clr   %o2
1ab4: 17 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o3
1ab8: d0 02 e0 58    ld    [%o3 + 0x58], %o0
1abc: d2 0b 00 0a    ldub  [%o4 + %o2], %o1
1ac0: 90 02 80 08    add   %o2, %o0, %o0
1ac4: 94 02 a0 01    inc   %o2
1ac8: 80 a2 a0 03    cmp   %o2, 3
1acc: 04 bf ff fb    ble   0x1ab8
1ad0: d2 2a 20 1c    stb   %o1, [%o0 + 0x1c] ! Copy four identifier bytes

1ad4: 11 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o0
1ad8: 98 12 20 48    or    %o0, 0x48, %o4   ! Configuration at 0x30003C48
1adc: 94 10 20 00    clr   %o2
1ae0: 17 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o3
1ae4: d0 02 e0 58    ld    [%o3 + 0x58], %o0
1ae8: d2 0b 00 0a    ldub  [%o4 + %o2], %o1
1aec: 90 02 80 08    add   %o2, %o0, %o0
1af0: 94 02 a0 01    inc   %o2
1af4: 80 a2 a0 03    cmp   %o2, 3
1af8: 04 bf ff fb    ble   0x1ae4
1afc: d2 2a 20 26    stb   %o1, [%o0 + 0x26] ! Copy four configuration bytes

1b00: 94 10 20 00    clr   %o2
1b04: 13 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o1
1b08: d0 02 60 5c    ld    [%o1 + 0x5c], %o0 ! Secondary buffer pointer
1b0c: c0 2a 00 0a    clrb  [%o0 + %o2]        ! Clear buffer byte
1b10: 94 02 a0 01    inc   %o2
1b14: 80 a2 a0 2a    cmp   %o2, 0x2a
1b18: 06 bf ff fd    bl    0x1b0c
1b1c: d0 02 60 5c    ld    [%o1 + 0x5c], %o0

1b20: d0 04 e0 58    ld    [%l3 + 0x58], %o0
1b24: 92 10 20 2a    mov   0x2a, %o1          ! Transfer length: 42
1b28: 7f ff fd ff    call  0x1324             ! First controller transfer
1b2c: 94 10 00 15    mov   %l5, %o2           ! Local result buffer
1b30: 80 a2 20 00    cmp   %o0, 0
1b34: 32 80 00 19    bne,a 0x1b98             ! Retry outer attempt on failure
1b38: a8 05 20 01    inc   %l4

1b3c: d0 04 e0 58    ld    [%l3 + 0x58], %o0
1b40: 92 10 20 2a    mov   0x2a, %o1
1b44: 7f ff fd f8    call  0x1324             ! Second controller transfer
1b48: 94 10 00 15    mov   %l5, %o2
1b4c: 80 a2 20 00    cmp   %o0, 0
1b50: 32 80 00 12    bne,a 0x1b98             ! Retry on failure
1b54: a8 05 20 01    inc   %l4

1b58: a0 10 20 01    mov   1, %l0              ! Inner attempt counter
1b5c: 25 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %l2
1b60: a2 07 bf e8    add   %fp, -24, %l1      ! Local response buffer
1b64: d0 04 a0 5c    ld    [%l2 + 0x5c], %o0 ! Secondary buffer
1b68: 92 10 28 00    mov   0x800, %o1         ! Transfer configuration
1b6c: 7f ff fe 4c    call  0x149c             ! Receive and validate response
1b70: 94 10 00 11    mov   %l1, %o2           ! Local result buffer
1b74: 80 a2 20 01    cmp   %o0, 1             ! Expected return value
1b78: 12 80 00 04    bne   0x1b88             ! Continue if not matched
1b7c: 90 10 00 10    mov   %l0, %o0           ! Prepare inner counter
1b80: 10 80 00 0a    b     0x1ba8             ! Success
1b84: b0 10 20 00    clr   %i0                ! Return 0
1b88: 80 a2 20 20    cmp   %o0, 0x20          ! Compare result with 32
1b8c: 04 bf ff f6    ble   0x1b64             ! Retry while condition holds
1b90: a0 04 20 01    inc   %l0                ! Increment counter (delay slot)
1b94: a8 05 20 01    inc   %l4                ! Next outer attempt
1b98: 80 a5 00 18    cmp   %l4, %i0
1b9c: 06 bf ff a6    bl    0x1a34             ! Retry from template copy
1ba0: 94 10 20 00    clr   %o2
1ba4: b0 10 20 02    mov   2, %i0              ! Attempts exhausted
1ba8: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
1bac: 81 e8 00 00    restore 
    
! 0x1BB0–0x1DAC: Build and transmit a network frame
! Steps:
! 1. Copy a 42-byte frame template into the transmit buffer.
! 2. Append the caller's payload at offset 0x2A.
! 3. Populate length, protocol and address fields.
! 4. Transmit the frame through routine 0x1324.
! 5. Optionally wait for a response through routine 0x149C.
!
! Arguments:
! %i0 = pointer to payload data
! %i1 = 16-bit field written at offsets 0x22–0x23
! %i2 = 16-bit field written at offsets 0x24–0x25
! %i3 = payload length
! %i4 = transmission/response flags
!
! RAM:
! 0x30003BA0 = 42-byte frame template
! 0x30003B40 = six-byte address
! 0x30003C40 = four-byte address
! 0x30003C48 = four-byte address
! 0x30003C50 = six-byte address
! 0x30003C58 = pointer to transmit buffer
! 0x30003C5C = pointer to receive buffer

1bb0: 9d e3 bf 88    save  %sp, -120, %sp
1bb4: 19 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o4
1bb8: 11 0c 00 0e    sethi %hi(0x30003800), %o0
1bbc: 9a 12 23 a0    or    %o0, 0x3a0, %o5   ! Frame template: 0x30003BA0
1bc0: 94 10 00 0c    mov   %o4, %o2
1bc4: 96 10 20 00    clr   %o3                ! Template copy index
1bc8: d2 0b 40 0b    ldub  [%o5 + %o3], %o1  ! Read template byte
1bcc: d0 02 a0 58    ld    [%o2 + 0x58], %o0 ! Load transmit buffer pointer
1bd0: d2 2a 00 0b    stb   %o1, [%o0 + %o3]  ! Copy byte to transmit buffer
1bd4: 96 02 e0 01    inc   %o3
1bd8: 80 a2 e0 29    cmp   %o3, 0x29         ! Last template offset: 41
1bdc: 24 bf ff fc    ble,a 0x1bcc            ! Continue copying 42 bytes
1be0: d2 0b 40 0b    ldub  [%o5 + %o3], %o1  ! Prefetch next byte (annulled slot)

! 0x1BE4–0x1C20: Append caller-supplied payload

1be4: 96 10 20 00    clr   %o3                ! Payload index
1be8: 80 a2 c0 1b    cmp   %o3, %i3           ! Check payload length
1bec: 9a 06 e0 08    add   %i3, 8, %o5       ! Length + 8
1bf0: 85 3e a0 08    sra   %i2, 8, %g2       ! High byte of argument 2
1bf4: 9f 3e 60 08    sra   %i1, 8, %o7       ! High byte of argument 1
1bf8: 82 06 e0 1c    add   %i3, 0x1c, %g1    ! Length + 28
1bfc: 16 80 00 0a    bge   0x1c24            ! Skip if payload length <= 0
1c00: 88 0f 20 01    and   %i4, 1, %g4       ! Extract flag bit 0
1c04: 15 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o2
1c08: d0 02 a0 58    ld    [%o2 + 0x58], %o0 ! Transmit buffer pointer
1c0c: d2 0e 00 0b    ldub  [%i0 + %o3], %o1  ! Read payload byte
1c10: 90 02 c0 08    add   %o3, %o0, %o0
1c14: 96 02 e0 01    inc   %o3
1c18: 80 a2 c0 1b    cmp   %o3, %i3
1c1c: 06 bf ff fb    bl    0x1c08            ! Copy remaining payload bytes
1c20: d2 2a 20 2a    stb   %o1, [%o0 + 0x2a] ! Write payload at offset 42

! 0x1C24–0x1C80: Populate protocol length and address fields

1c24: d2 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o1
1c28: 91 3b 60 08    sra   %o5, 8, %o0
1c2c: d0 2a 60 26    stb   %o0, [%o1 + 0x26] ! UDP length high byte
1c30: d4 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o2
1c34: 13 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o1
1c38: da 2a a0 27    stb   %o5, [%o2 + 0x27] ! UDP length low byte
1c3c: d0 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o0
1c40: 86 12 60 48    or    %o1, 0x48, %g3   ! Stored IPv4 address
1c44: c4 2a 20 24    stb   %g2, [%o0 + 0x24] ! UDP field high byte
1c48: d2 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o1
1c4c: 96 10 20 00    clr   %o3
1c50: f4 2a 60 25    stb   %i2, [%o1 + 0x25] ! UDP field low byte
1c54: d4 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o2
1c58: 9a 10 00 0c    mov   %o4, %o5
1c5c: de 2a a0 22    stb   %o7, [%o2 + 0x22] ! UDP field high byte
1c60: d0 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o0
1c64: f2 2a 20 23    stb   %i1, [%o0 + 0x23] ! UDP field low byte
1c68: d0 03 60 58    ld    [%o5 + 0x58], %o0
1c6c: d2 08 c0 0b    ldub  [%g3 + %o3], %o1 ! Read IPv4 address byte
1c70: 90 02 c0 08    add   %o3, %o0, %o0
1c74: 96 02 e0 01    inc   %o3
1c78: 80 a2 e0 03    cmp   %o3, 3
1c7c: 04 bf ff fb    ble   0x1c68
1c80: d2 2a 20 1e    stb   %o1, [%o0 + 0x1e] ! Copy four IPv4 address bytes

! 0x1C84–0x1D18: Populate remaining frame header fields

1c84: 11 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o0
1c88: 9a 12 20 40    or    %o0, 0x40, %o5   ! Stored IPv4 address
1c8c: 96 10 20 00    clr   %o3
1c90: 15 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o2
1c94: d0 02 a0 58    ld    [%o2 + 0x58], %o0
1c98: d2 0b 40 0b    ldub  [%o5 + %o3], %o1
1c9c: 90 02 c0 08    add   %o3, %o0, %o0
1ca0: 96 02 e0 01    inc   %o3
1ca4: 80 a2 e0 03    cmp   %o3, 3
1ca8: 04 bf ff fb    ble   0x1c94
1cac: d2 2a 20 1a    stb   %o1, [%o0 + 0x1a] ! Copy four address bytes
1cb0: d2 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o1
1cb4: 91 38 60 08    sra   %g1, 8, %o0
1cb8: d0 2a 60 10    stb   %o0, [%o1 + 0x10] ! IP total length high byte
1cbc: d4 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o2
1cc0: 11 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o0
1cc4: c2 2a a0 11    stb   %g1, [%o2 + 0x11] ! IP total length low byte

! 0x1CC8–0x1D18: Copy MAC addresses

1cc8: 9a 12 20 50    or    %o0, 0x50, %o5   ! MAC address at 0x30003C50
1ccc: 96 10 20 00    clr   %o3
1cd0: 94 10 00 0c    mov   %o4, %o2
1cd4: d2 0b 40 0b    ldub  [%o5 + %o3], %o1
1cd8: d0 02 a0 58    ld    [%o2 + 0x58], %o0
1cdc: d2 2a 00 0b    stb   %o1, [%o0 + %o3]  ! Destination MAC byte
1ce0: 96 02 e0 01    inc   %o3
1ce4: 80 a2 e0 05    cmp   %o3, 5
1ce8: 24 bf ff fc    ble,a 0x1cd8
1cec: d2 0b 40 0b    ldub  [%o5 + %o3], %o1

1cf0: 11 0c 00 0e    sethi %hi(0x30003800), %o0
1cf4: 9a 12 23 40    or    %o0, 0x340, %o5 ! MAC address at 0x30003B40
1cf8: 96 10 20 00    clr   %o3
1cfc: 15 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %o2
1d00: d0 02 a0 58    ld    [%o2 + 0x58], %o0
1d04: d2 0b 40 0b    ldub  [%o5 + %o3], %o1
1d08: 90 02 c0 08    add   %o3, %o0, %o0
1d0c: 96 02 e0 01    inc   %o3
1d10: 80 a2 e0 05    cmp   %o3, 5
1d14: 04 bf ff fb    ble   0x1d00
1d18: d2 2a 20 06    stb   %o1, [%o0 + 6]  ! Source MAC byte

! 0x1D1C–0x1DAC: Transmit frame and optionally await a response

1d1c: 80 a1 20 00    cmp   %g4, 0             ! Test flag bit 0
1d20: 12 80 00 09    bne   0x1d44             ! Use response-handling path
1d24: 80 8f 20 02    btst  2, %i4             ! Test flag bit 1 (delay slot)
1d28: d0 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o0 ! Transmit buffer
1d2c: 92 06 e0 2a    add   %i3, 0x2a, %o1    ! Total frame length
1d30: 7f ff fd 7d    call  0x1324             ! Transmit frame
1d34: 94 07 bf f0    add   %fp, -16, %o2     ! Local result buffer
1d38: 80 a0 00 08    cmp   %g0, %o0           ! Compare return value with zero
1d3c: 10 80 00 1b    b     0x1da8
1d40: b0 40 20 00    addx  %g0, 0, %i0       ! Return 1 if transfer returned < 0

1d44: 02 80 00 0b    be    0x1d70             ! Skip transmit when flag bit 1 clear
1d48: d0 03 20 58    ld    [%o4 + 0x58], %o0
1d4c: 92 06 e0 2a    add   %i3, 0x2a, %o1
1d50: 7f ff fd 75    call  0x1324             ! Transmit frame
1d54: 94 07 bf f0    add   %fp, -16, %o2
1d58: 80 a2 20 00    cmp   %o0, 0
1d5c: 02 80 00 05    be    0x1d70             ! Transfer successful
1d60: b0 10 20 01    mov   1, %i0             ! Prepare failure return
1d64: 30 80 00 11    b,a   0x1da8             ! Return on transfer failure
1d68: 10 80 00 10    b     0x1da8
1d6c: b0 10 20 00    clr   %i0                ! Return 0
1d70: b0 10 20 01    mov   1, %i0             ! Response attempt counter
1d74: 35 0c 00 0f    sethi %hi(0x30003c00), %i2
1d78: b2 07 bf e8    add   %fp, -24, %i1     ! Local response buffer
1d7c: d0 06 a0 5c    ld    [%i2 + 0x5c], %o0 ! Receive buffer pointer
1d80: 92 10 28 00    mov   0x800, %o1
1d84: 7f ff fd c6    call  0x149c             ! Receive and inspect response
1d88: 94 10 00 19    mov   %i1, %o2
1d8c: 80 a2 20 03    cmp   %o0, 3             ! Expected response status
1d90: 02 bf ff f6    be    0x1d68             ! Return success if matched
1d94: 90 10 00 18    mov   %i0, %o0           ! Delay slot: attempt counter
1d98: 80 a2 20 20    cmp   %o0, 0x20
1d9c: 04 bf ff f8    ble   0x1d7c             ! Retry up to 32 attempts
1da0: b0 06 20 01    inc   %i0                ! Increment attempt counter
1da4: b0 10 20 01    mov   1, %i0             ! Return failure
1da8: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
1dac: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0x1DB0: Initialize network controller and transfer buffers
! 1. Reset controller state and configure MMIO.
! 2. Assign transmit and receive buffers.
! 3. Validate arguments and parse network addresses.

1db0: 9d e3 be 98 save  %sp, -360, %sp                  ! Allocate 360-byte stack frame
1db4: 15 08 00 0a sethi  %hi(0x20002800), %o2          
1db8: 90 10 20 0b mov  0xb, %o0                        
1dbc: d0 22 80 00 st  %o0, [ %o2 ]                      ! Write 0x0B to MMIO 0x20002800
1dc0: 1b 08 00 41 sethi  %hi(0x20010400), %o5          
1dc4: d0 03 40 00 ld  [ %o5 ], %o0                      ! Read controller control register
1dc8: 13 00 00 20 sethi  %hi(0x8000), %o1              
1dcc: 90 12 20 40 or  %o0, 0x40, %o0                    ! Set bit 6
1dd0: d0 23 40 00 st  %o0, [ %o5 ]                     
1dd4: 92 12 60 0b or  %o1, 0xb, %o1                    
1dd8: d2 22 80 00 st  %o1, [ %o2 ]                      ! Write 0x800B to MMIO
1ddc: d2 03 40 00 ld  [ %o5 ], %o1                     
1de0: 19 0c 00 0e sethi  %hi(0x30003800), %o4          
1de4: d6 03 23 48 ld  [ %o4 + 0x348 ], %o3             
1de8: 15 0c 00 0f sethi  %hi(0x30003c00), %o2          
1dec: 11 21 e0 02 sethi  %hi(0x87800800), %o0          
1df0: 92 0a 7f bf and  %o1, -65, %o1                    ! Clear bit 6
1df4: d0 22 a0 58 st  %o0, [ %o2 + 0x58 ]               ! Set transmit buffer pointer to 0x87800800
1df8: 07 0c 00 0f sethi  %hi(0x30003c00), %g3          
1dfc: d2 23 40 00 st  %o1, [ %o5 ]                     
1e00: 96 02 e0 01 inc  %o3                             
1e04: 05 21 e0 00 sethi  %hi(0x87800000), %g2          
1e08: aa 10 00 0c mov  %o4, %l5                        
1e0c: ac 10 00 03 mov  %g3, %l6                        
1e10: d6 23 23 48 st  %o3, [ %o4 + 0x348 ]              ! Update persistent counter
1e14: 80 a6 20 06 cmp  %i0, 6                           ! Require six arguments
1e18: 12 80 00 3c bne  0x1f08                          
1e1c: c4 20 e0 5c st  %g2, [ %g3 + 0x5c ]               ! Set receive buffer pointer to 0x87800000 (delay slot)

! 0x1E20: Parse MAC address, IPv4 addresses, and numeric argument
! 1. Parse colon-separated MAC address.
! 2. Parse two dot-separated IPv4 addresses.
! 3. Convert numeric argument and inspect stored network configuration.

1e20: 11 0c 00 0e sethi  %hi(0x30003800), %o0          
1e24: d2 06 60 04 ld  [ %i1 + 4 ], %o1                  ! Load MAC address string
1e28: a6 12 23 40 or  %o0, 0x340, %l3                  
1e2c: 7f ff fe cf call  0x1968                          ! Parse colon-separated hexadecimal bytes
1e30: 90 10 00 13 mov  %l3, %o0                        
1e34: 23 0c 00 0f sethi  %hi(0x30003c00), %l1          
1e38: d2 06 60 08 ld  [ %i1 + 8 ], %o1                  ! Load first IPv4 address string
1e3c: a0 14 60 40 or  %l1, 0x40, %l0                   
1e40: 7f ff fe af call  0x18fc                          ! Parse dotted-decimal bytes
1e44: 90 10 00 10 mov  %l0, %o0                        
1e48: d2 06 60 0c ld  [ %i1 + 0xc ], %o1                ! Load second IPv4 address string
1e4c: 11 0c 00 0f sethi  %hi(0x30003c00), %o0          
1e50: 7f ff fe ab call  0x18fc                          ! Parse dotted-decimal bytes
1e54: 90 12 20 48 or  %o0, 0x48, %o0                   
1e58: ee 06 60 10 ld  [ %i1 + 0x10 ], %l7               ! Save pointer to request string
1e5c: 92 10 20 00 clr  %o1                             
1e60: d0 06 60 14 ld  [ %i1 + 0x14 ], %o0               ! Load numeric argument string
1e64: 7f ff fa 83 call  0x870                           ! Convert string to integer
1e68: 94 10 20 00 clr  %o2                             
1e6c: d2 0c 20 02 ldub  [ %l0 + 2 ], %o1               
1e70: b0 10 00 08 mov  %o0, %i0                        
1e74: 80 a2 60 7d cmp  %o1, 0x7d                        ! Check address byte against 0x7D
1e78: 12 80 00 26 bne  0x1f10                          
1e7c: a4 10 00 18 mov  %i0, %l2                        

! 0x1E80: Inspect stored network configuration
! 1. Compare bytes and update selected network address fields.

1e80: 11 0c 00 0f sethi  %hi(0x30003c00), %o0          
1e84: d2 0a 20 80 ldub  [ %o0 + 0x80 ], %o1             ! Read stored configuration byte
1e88: 98 10 00 08 mov  %o0, %o4                        
1e8c: 80 a2 60 00 cmp  %o1, 0                          
1e90: 12 80 00 20 bne  0x1f10                          
1e94: 94 12 20 80 or  %o0, 0x80, %o2                   
1e98: d0 0a a0 01 ldub  [ %o2 + 1 ], %o0               
1e9c: 80 a2 20 0d cmp  %o0, 0xd                         ! Check byte against 0x0D
1ea0: 12 80 00 1c bne  0x1f10                          
1ea4: 01 00 00 00 nop                                  
1ea8: d0 0a a0 02 ldub  [ %o2 + 2 ], %o0               
1eac: 80 a2 20 a2 cmp  %o0, 0xa2                        ! Check byte against 0xA2
1eb0: 12 80 00 18 bne  0x1f10                          
1eb4: 92 10 00 0a mov  %o2, %o1                        
1eb8: 96 10 00 13 mov  %l3, %o3                        
1ebc: 94 10 20 00 clr  %o2                             
1ec0: d0 0a 40 0a ldub  [ %o1 + %o2 ], %o0              ! Copy byte from stored configuration
1ec4: d0 2a c0 0a stb  %o0, [ %o3 + %o2 ]              
1ec8: 94 02 a0 01 inc  %o2                             
1ecc: 80 a2 a0 05 cmp  %o2, 5                          
1ed0: 24 bf ff fd ble,a   0x1ec4                        ! Repeat for six bytes
1ed4: d0 0a 40 0a ldub  [ %o1 + %o2 ], %o0             
1ed8: 90 13 20 80 or  %o4, 0x80, %o0                   
1edc: d0 0a 20 05 ldub  [ %o0 + 5 ], %o0               
1ee0: 92 0a 20 ff and  %o0, 0xff, %o1                  
1ee4: 80 a2 60 09 cmp  %o1, 9                           ! Compare byte with 9
1ee8: 08 80 00 05 bleu  0x1efc                         
1eec: a2 14 60 40 or  %l1, 0x40, %l1                   
1ef0: 80 a2 60 fd cmp  %o1, 0xfd                        ! Compare byte with 0xFD
1ef4: 28 80 00 07 bleu,a   0x1f10                      
1ef8: d0 2c 60 03 stb  %o0, [ %l1 + 3 ]                
1efc: 90 02 3f 80 add  %o0, -128, %o0                  
1f00: 10 80 00 04 b  0x1f10                            
1f04: d0 2c 60 03 stb  %o0, [ %l1 + 3 ]                
1f08: 10 80 00 a7 b  0x21a4                             ! Return status 1 for invalid argument count
1f0c: b0 10 20 01 mov  1, %i0                          

! 0x1F10: Perform network setup and prepare a request
! 1. Run setup routine 0x1A0C.
! 2. Build a request payload containing a caller-supplied string and mode suffix.
! 3. Transmit the request through 0x1BB0.

1f10: 7f ff fe bf call  0x1a0c                          ! Initialize network via 0x1A0C
1f14: 90 10 20 0a mov  0xa, %o0                        
1f18: 80 a2 20 00 cmp  %o0, 0                           ! Test initialization result
1f1c: 02 80 00 04 be  0x1f2c                           
1f20: 90 07 be f8 add  %fp, -264, %o0                  
1f24: 10 80 00 a0 b  0x21a4                             ! Return status 2 if initialization failed
1f28: b0 10 20 02 mov  2, %i0                          
1f2c: 94 10 20 01 mov  1, %o2                           ! Initialize local request buffer
1f30: c0 2a 00 00 clrb  [ %o0 ]                        
1f34: b2 10 00 08 mov  %o0, %i1                        
1f38: d4 2a 00 0a stb  %o2, [ %o0 + %o2 ]              
1f3c: 98 10 00 19 mov  %i1, %o4                        
1f40: 96 10 00 17 mov  %l7, %o3                        
1f44: 94 10 20 02 mov  2, %o2                          
1f48: d0 0a c0 00 ldub  [ %o3 ], %o0                    ! Read request string byte
1f4c: 93 2a 20 18 sll  %o0, 0x18, %o1                  
1f50: d0 2b 00 0a stb  %o0, [ %o4 + %o2 ]              
1f54: 80 a2 60 00 cmp  %o1, 0                          
1f58: 96 02 e0 01 inc  %o3                             
1f5c: 12 bf ff fb bne  0x1f48                           ! Continue until NUL
1f60: 94 02 a0 01 inc  %o2                             
1f64: 90 10 20 6f mov  0x6f, %o0                        ! Append 'o'
1f68: d0 2e 40 0a stb  %o0, [ %i1 + %o2 ]              
1f6c: 94 02 a0 01 inc  %o2                             
1f70: 90 10 20 63 mov  0x63, %o0                        ! Append 'c'
1f74: d0 2e 40 0a stb  %o0, [ %i1 + %o2 ]              
1f78: 94 02 a0 01 inc  %o2                             
1f7c: 92 10 20 74 mov  0x74, %o1                        ! Append 't'
1f80: d2 2e 40 0a stb  %o1, [ %i1 + %o2 ]              
1f84: 94 02 a0 01 inc  %o2                             
1f88: 90 10 20 65 mov  0x65, %o0                        ! Append 'e'
1f8c: d0 2e 40 0a stb  %o0, [ %i1 + %o2 ]              
1f90: 94 02 a0 01 inc  %o2                             
1f94: d2 2e 40 0a stb  %o1, [ %i1 + %o2 ]               ! Append 't'
1f98: d2 05 63 48 ld  [ %l5 + 0x348 ], %o1             
1f9c: 94 02 a0 01 inc  %o2                             
1fa0: 96 02 a0 01 add  %o2, 1, %o3                     
1fa4: c0 2e 40 0a clrb  [ %i1 + %o2 ]                   ! Append NUL terminator
1fa8: 90 10 00 19 mov  %i1, %o0                        
1fac: 94 10 20 45 mov  0x45, %o2                       
1fb0: 7f ff ff 00 call  0x1bb0                          ! Send request using 0x1BB0
1fb4: 98 10 20 03 mov  3, %o4                           ! Flags = 3 (delay slot)
1fb8: 80 a2 20 00 cmp  %o0, 0                          
1fbc: 02 80 00 06 be  0x1fd4                           
1fc0: 11 0c 00 0e sethi  %hi(0x30003800), %o0          
1fc4: 7f ff fa 3a call  0x8ac                           ! Print error message
1fc8: 90 12 21 70 or  %o0, 0x170, %o0                  
1fcc: 10 80 00 76 b  0x21a4                             ! Return status 3
1fd0: b0 10 20 03 mov  3, %i0                          

! 0x1FD4: Receive and validate numbered data blocks
! 1. Check response opcode and block number.
! 2. Request retransmission when a block is missing.
! 3. Copy received data to destination.

1fd4: a2 10 20 00 clr  %l1                             
1fd8: a6 10 20 01 mov  1, %l3                          
1fdc: d6 05 a0 5c ld  [ %l6 + 0x5c ], %o3               ! Load receive buffer pointer
1fe0: d0 0a e0 2a ldub  [ %o3 + 0x2a ], %o0             ! Read response payload opcode high byte
1fe4: 80 a2 20 00 cmp  %o0, 0                          
1fe8: 12 80 00 06 bne  0x2000                          
1fec: 11 0c 00 0e sethi  %hi(0x30003800), %o0          
1ff0: d0 0a e0 2b ldub  [ %o3 + 0x2b ], %o0             ! Read response payload opcode low byte
1ff4: 80 a2 20 03 cmp  %o0, 3                          
1ff8: 02 80 00 06 be  0x2010                            ! Check opcode 3 (DATA)
1ffc: 11 0c 00 0e sethi  %hi(0x30003800), %o0          
2000: 7f ff fa 2b call  0x8ac                           ! Print unexpected response message
2004: 90 12 21 78 or  %o0, 0x178, %o0                  
2008: 10 80 00 67 b  0x21a4                             ! Return status 4
200c: b0 10 20 04 mov  4, %i0                          
2010: d0 0a e0 2c ldub  [ %o3 + 0x2c ], %o0             ! Read block number high byte
2014: d2 0a e0 2d ldub  [ %o3 + 0x2d ], %o1             ! Read block number low byte
2018: 91 2a 20 08 sll  %o0, 8, %o0                     
201c: 94 12 00 09 or  %o0, %o1, %o2                    
2020: 80 a2 80 13 cmp  %o2, %l3                         ! Compare received block number with expected
2024: 16 80 00 09 bge  0x2048                          
2028: d2 05 63 48 ld  [ %l5 + 0x348 ], %o1             
202c: 90 10 00 19 mov  %i1, %o0                         ! Prepare retransmission request
2030: 94 10 00 14 mov  %l4, %o2                        
2034: 96 10 20 04 mov  4, %o3                          
2038: 7f ff fe de call  0x1bb0                          ! Send response using 0x1BB0
203c: 98 10 20 01 mov  1, %o4                          
2040: 10 bf ff e8 b  0x1fe0                             ! Check received response again
2044: d6 05 a0 5c ld  [ %l6 + 0x5c ], %o3              
2048: 24 80 00 04 ble,a   0x2058                       
204c: d0 0a e0 26 ldub  [ %o3 + 0x26 ], %o0            
2050: 10 80 00 55 b  0x21a4                             ! Return status 5 for unexpected block number
2054: b0 10 20 05 mov  5, %i0                          
2058: 15 3f fc 00 sethi  %hi(0xfff00000), %o2           ! Read UDP length high byte
205c: d2 0a e0 27 ldub  [ %o3 + 0x27 ], %o1            
2060: 91 2a 20 08 sll  %o0, 8, %o0                     
2064: a0 12 00 09 or  %o0, %o1, %l0                     ! Combine UDP length bytes
2068: a0 04 3f f4 add  %l0, -12, %l0                    ! Subtract UDP header (8) and TFTP header (4)
206c: a2 04 40 10 add  %l1, %l0, %l1                    ! Add data length to accumulated total
2070: 80 ac 40 0a andncc  %l1, %o2, %g0                 ! Test accumulated byte count against 0xFFF00000 mask
2074: 12 80 00 07 bne  0x2090                          
2078: a6 04 e0 01 inc  %l3                             
207c: 7f ff fa 2e call  0x934                           ! Print upper byte of total
2080: 91 34 60 14 srl  %l1, 0x14, %o0                  
2084: 11 0c 00 0e sethi  %hi(0x30003800), %o0          
2088: 7f ff fa 09 call  0x8ac                           ! Print progress message
208c: 90 12 21 80 or  %o0, 0x180, %o0                  

! 0x2090: Copy payload data and acknowledge received block
! 1. Copy payload in 16-bit units.
! 2. Build acknowledgment payload and send through 0x1BB0.

2090: 94 10 20 00 clr  %o2                             
2094: 80 a2 80 10 cmp  %o2, %l0                         ! Check whether data remains to copy
2098: 36 80 00 0c bge,a   0x20c8                       
209c: d4 05 a0 5c ld  [ %l6 + 0x5c ], %o2              
20a0: 96 10 00 16 mov  %l6, %o3                        
20a4: d0 02 e0 5c ld  [ %o3 + 0x5c ], %o0              
20a8: 90 02 80 08 add  %o2, %o0, %o0                   
20ac: d2 12 20 2e lduh  [ %o0 + 0x2e ], %o1             ! Read 16-bit data from receive buffer + 0x2E
20b0: 94 02 a0 02 add  %o2, 2, %o2                     
20b4: d2 34 80 00 sth  %o1, [ %l2 ]                     ! Write 16-bit word to destination
20b8: 80 a2 80 10 cmp  %o2, %l0                        
20bc: 06 bf ff fa bl  0x20a4                           
20c0: a4 04 a0 02 add  %l2, 2, %l2                      ! Advance destination pointer by two bytes (delay slot)
20c4: d4 05 a0 5c ld  [ %l6 + 0x5c ], %o2              
20c8: 90 10 20 04 mov  4, %o0                           ! Set ACK opcode low byte to 4
20cc: d0 2f be f9 stb  %o0, [ %fp + -263 ]             
20d0: c0 2f be f8 clrb  [ %fp + -264 ]                 
20d4: d2 0a a0 2c ldub  [ %o2 + 0x2c ], %o1             ! Read block number high byte
20d8: 80 a4 21 ff cmp  %l0, 0x1ff                      
20dc: d2 2f be fa stb  %o1, [ %fp + -262 ]             
20e0: d0 0a a0 2d ldub  [ %o2 + 0x2d ], %o0             ! Read block number low byte
20e4: d0 2f be fb stb  %o0, [ %fp + -261 ]             
20e8: d2 0a a0 22 ldub  [ %o2 + 0x22 ], %o1             ! Read received source UDP port high byte
20ec: d0 0a a0 23 ldub  [ %o2 + 0x23 ], %o0             ! Read received source UDP port low byte
20f0: 93 2a 60 08 sll  %o1, 8, %o1                     
20f4: 14 80 00 22 bg  0x217c                            ! Branch if data length > 511
20f8: a8 12 40 08 or  %o1, %o0, %l4                    
20fc: d2 05 63 48 ld  [ %l5 + 0x348 ], %o1              ! Load current transaction counter
2100: 90 10 00 19 mov  %i1, %o0                        
2104: 94 10 00 14 mov  %l4, %o2                        
2108: 96 10 20 04 mov  4, %o3

! 0x21AC: Flash address translation
