2fb4: 81 e8 00 00    restore

! 0x2FB8–0x3004: Wait for hardware status bit 0 to clear
! Steps:
! 1. Read the status register at 0x200000E0.
! 2. If bit 0 is clear, return immediately.
! 3. Otherwise poll with a bounded counter.
! 4. Call the error handler at 0x368C if the counter expires.
! 5. Return once the status bit clears.

2fb8: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp
2fbc: 11 08 00 00    sethi %hi(0x20000000), %o0
2fc0: 94 12 20 e0    or    %o0, 0xe0, %o2     ! Status register 0x200000E0
2fc4: d2 02 80 00    ld    [ %o2 ], %o1
2fc8: 11 00 01 24    sethi %hi(0x49000), %o0
2fcc: 80 8a 60 01    btst  1, %o1
2fd0: 02 80 00 0c    be    0x3000             ! Already ready
2fd4: a0 12 23 e0    or    %o0, 0x3e0, %l0    ! Counter = 0x493E0
2fd8: a2 10 00 0a    mov   %o2, %l1
2fdc: a0 84 3f ff    addcc %l0, -1, %l0
2fe0: 12 80 00 04    bne   0x2ff0
2fe4: 90 10 20 01    mov   1, %o0
2fe8: 40 00 01 a9    call  0x368c             ! Timeout/error handler
2fec: 92 10 20 0c    mov   0xc, %o1
2ff0: d0 04 40 00    ld    [ %l1 ], %o0
2ff4: 80 8a 20 01    btst  1, %o0
2ff8: 12 bf ff fa    bne   0x2fe0             ! Continue while bit 0 is set
2ffc: a0 84 3f ff    addcc %l0, -1, %l0      ! Counter decremented again

3000: 81 c7 e0 08    ret
3004: 81 e8 00 00    restore


! 0x3008: MMIO transfer write (four arguments)
! Write two data words and a packed command; wait for completion.
    3008:	9d e3 bf 98 	save  %sp, -104, %sp
    300c:	13 08 00 00 	sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %o1
    3010:	90 12 60 d8 	or  %o1, 0xd8, %o0	! 0x200000d8    ! MMIO data register 0x200000D8
    3014:	b6 06 ff ff 	add  %i3, -1, %i3
    3018:	f2 22 00 00 	st  %i1, [ %o0 ]    ! Write first data word
    301c:	b1 2e 20 01 	sll  %i0, 1, %i0
    3020:	b7 2e e0 08 	sll  %i3, 8, %i3    ! Pack (count - 1) into command bits
    3024:	15 00 00 0a 	sethi  %hi(0x2800), %o2
    3028:	90 12 60 dc 	or  %o1, 0xdc, %o0
    302c:	b6 16 c0 0a 	or  %i3, %o2, %i3    ! Add fixed command flags 0x2800
    3030:	b0 0e 20 fe 	and  %i0, 0xfe, %i0
    3034:	b0 16 00 1b 	or  %i0, %i3, %i0
    3038:	f4 22 00 00 	st  %i2, [ %o0 ]    ! Write second data word to 0x200000DC
    303c:	92 12 60 d0 	or  %o1, 0xd0, %o1
    3040:	f0 22 40 00 	st  %i0, [ %o1 ]    ! Start transfer via 0x200000D0
    3044:	7f ff ff dd 	call  0x2fb8    ! Wait until MMIO status bit 0 clears
    3048:	01 00 00 00 	nop 
    304c:	81 c7 e0 08 	ret 
    3050:	81 e8 00 00 	restore 

! 0x3054: MMIO transfer read
! Issue a read command; store two returned words through caller pointers.
    3054:	9d e3 bf 98 	save  %sp, -104, %sp
    3058:	b6 06 ff ff 	add  %i3, -1, %i3    ! Convert count to encoded (count - 1) field
    305c:	b7 2e e0 08 	sll  %i3, 8, %i3
    3060:	11 00 00 0a 	sethi  %hi(0x2800), %o0
    3064:	b1 2e 20 01 	sll  %i0, 1, %i0
    3068:	b6 16 c0 08 	or  %i3, %o0, %i3
    306c:	b0 0e 20 fe 	and  %i0, 0xfe, %i0
    3070:	21 08 00 00 	sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %l0
    3074:	b0 16 00 1b 	or  %i0, %i3, %i0
    3078:	90 14 20 d0 	or  %l0, 0xd0, %o0
    307c:	b0 16 20 01 	or  %i0, 1, %i0    ! Set read-direction bit 0
    3080:	f0 22 00 00 	st  %i0, [ %o0 ]    ! Start read command
    3084:	7f ff ff cd 	call  0x2fb8    ! Wait for transfer completion
    3088:	01 00 00 00 	nop 
    308c:	90 14 20 d8 	or  %l0, 0xd8, %o0
    3090:	d2 02 00 00 	ld  [ %o0 ], %o1    ! Read first returned word
    3094:	a0 14 20 dc 	or  %l0, 0xdc, %l0
    3098:	d2 26 40 00 	st  %o1, [ %i1 ]    ! Store first word through pointer i1
    309c:	d0 04 00 00 	ld  [ %l0 ], %o0    ! Read second returned word
    30a0:	d0 26 80 00 	st  %o0, [ %i2 ]    ! Store second word through pointer i2
    30a4:	81 c7 e0 08 	ret 
    30a8:	81 e8 00 00 	restore 

! 0x30AC: MMIO status query
! Issue a command and return a Boolean derived from status bit 1.
    30ac:	9d e3 bf 98 	save  %sp, -104, %sp
    30b0:	11 00 00 0a 	sethi  %hi(0x2800), %o0
    30b4:	90 12 20 01 	or  %o0, 1, %o0	! 0x2801    ! Command base 0x2801
    30b8:	b1 2e 20 01 	sll  %i0, 1, %i0
    30bc:	21 08 00 00 	sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %l0
    30c0:	b0 16 00 08 	or  %i0, %o0, %i0
    30c4:	92 14 20 d0 	or  %l0, 0xd0, %o1
    30c8:	f0 22 40 00 	st  %i0, [ %o1 ]    ! Start command
    30cc:	7f ff ff bb 	call  0x2fb8    ! Wait for completion
    30d0:	a0 14 20 e0 	or  %l0, 0xe0, %l0
    30d4:	d0 04 00 00 	ld  [ %l0 ], %o0    ! Read status register 0x200000E0
    30d8:	90 0a 20 02 	and  %o0, 2, %o0    ! Extract status bit 1
    30dc:	80 a0 00 08 	cmp  %g0, %o0
    30e0:	b0 60 3f ff 	subx  %g0, -1, %i0    ! Return 1 if bit set, 0 otherwise (carry-based)
    30e4:	81 c7 e0 08 	ret 
    30e8:	81 e8 00 00 	restore 

! 0x30EC: Hardware setup and external firmware call
! Initialize registers, choose one of two external entry points, store returned registers.
    30ec:	9d e3 bf 98 	save  %sp, -104, %sp
    30f0:	07 08 00 0c 	sethi  %hi(0x20003000), %g3
    30f4:	84 10 20 01 	mov  1, %g2
    30f8:	c4 20 c0 00 	st  %g2, [ %g3 ]    ! Write 1 to 0x20003000
    30fc:	b2 10 20 02 	mov  2, %i1
    3100:	f2 20 c0 00 	st  %i1, [ %g3 ]    ! Write 2 to 0x20003000
    3104:	86 10 e0 04 	or  %g3, 4, %g3
    3108:	84 10 20 0a 	mov  0xa, %g2
    310c:	33 0c 00 0f 	sethi  %hi(0x30003c00), %i1
    3110:	c4 20 c0 00 	st  %g2, [ %g3 ]    ! Write 10 to 0x20003004
    3114:	80 8e 20 ff 	btst  0xff, %i0
    3118:	02 80 00 05 	be  0x312c
    311c:	f0 2e 60 7c 	stb  %i0, [ %i1 + 0x7c ]    ! Save low byte of argument to 0x30003C7C (delay slot)
    3120:	05 10 00 00 	sethi  %hi(0x40000000), %g2
    3124:	10 80 00 04 	b  0x3134
    3128:	86 10 a3 f8 	or  %g2, 0x3f8, %g3	! 0x400003f8    ! External entry 0x400003F8 for nonzero low byte
    312c:	05 10 00 00 	sethi  %hi(0x40000000), %g2
    3130:	86 10 a3 f0 	or  %g2, 0x3f0, %g3	! 0x400003f0    ! External entry 0x400003F0 for zero low byte
    3134:	05 08 00 0c 	sethi  %hi(0x20003000), %g2
    3138:	84 10 a0 08 	or  %g2, 8, %g2	! 0x20003008
    313c:	9f c0 c0 00 	call  %g3    ! Call firmware/ROM entry at 0x400003F0 or 0x400003F8
    3140:	01 00 00 00 	nop 
    3144:	d0 20 a0 00 	st  %o0, [ %g2 ]    ! Save returned register o0 to MMIO 0x20003008
    3148:	d2 20 a0 04 	st  %o1, [ %g2 + 4 ]    ! Save returned register o1
    314c:	d4 20 a0 08 	st  %o2, [ %g2 + 8 ]    ! Save returned register o2
    3150:	d6 20 a0 0c 	st  %o3, [ %g2 + 0xc ]    ! Save returned register o3
    3154:	d8 20 a0 10 	st  %o4, [ %g2 + 0x10 ]    ! Save returned register o4
    3158:	da 20 a0 14 	st  %o5, [ %g2 + 0x14 ]    ! Save returned register o5
    315c:	31 08 00 0a 	sethi  %hi(0x20002800), %i0
    3160:	84 16 20 c4 	or  %i0, 0xc4, %g2	! 0x200028c4
    3164:	c0 20 80 00 	clr  [ %g2 ]    ! Clear 0x200028C4
    3168:	b2 16 20 d4 	or  %i0, 0xd4, %i1
    316c:	07 00 00 20 	sethi  %hi(0x8000), %g3
    3170:	c0 26 40 00 	clr  [ %i1 ]    ! Clear 0x200028D4
    3174:	86 10 e0 0a 	or  %g3, 0xa, %g3
    3178:	84 16 20 c0 	or  %i0, 0xc0, %g2
    317c:	c6 20 80 00 	st  %g3, [ %g2 ]    ! Write 0x800A to 0x200028C0
    3180:	b0 16 20 d0 	or  %i0, 0xd0, %i0
    3184:	c6 26 00 00 	st  %g3, [ %i0 ]    ! Write 0x800A to 0x200028D0
    3188:	81 c7 e0 08 	ret 
    318c:	81 e8 00 00 	restore 

! 0x3190: Hardware operation and optional XOR transformation
! Program hardware, poll status with timeout, optionally XOR a buffer in 32-bit words.
    3190:	9d e3 bf 98 	save  %sp, -104, %sp
    3194:	15 08 00 0a 	sethi  %hi(0x20002800), %o2
    3198:	17 30 00 00 	sethi  %hi(0xc0000000), %o3
    319c:	b0 06 00 0b 	add  %i0, %o3, %i0    ! Translate first address with +0xC0000000
    31a0:	90 12 a0 d8 	or  %o2, 0xd8, %o0
    31a4:	f0 22 00 00 	st  %i0, [ %o0 ]    ! Write translated address to 0x200028D8
    31a8:	90 12 a0 c8 	or  %o2, 0xc8, %o0
    31ac:	96 06 40 0b 	add  %i1, %o3, %o3    ! Translate second address
    31b0:	d6 22 00 00 	st  %o3, [ %o0 ]    ! Write translated address to 0x200028C8
    31b4:	13 00 80 00 	sethi  %hi(0x2000000), %o1
    31b8:	98 12 a0 c0 	or  %o2, 0xc0, %o4
    31bc:	92 16 80 09 	or  %i2, %o1, %o1
    31c0:	96 12 a0 dc 	or  %o2, 0xdc, %o3
    31c4:	11 00 c0 00 	sethi  %hi(0x3000000), %o0
    31c8:	d2 22 c0 00 	st  %o1, [ %o3 ]    ! Write length/flags to 0x200028DC
    31cc:	94 12 a0 cc 	or  %o2, 0xcc, %o2
    31d0:	90 16 80 08 	or  %i2, %o0, %o0
    31d4:	d0 22 80 00 	st  %o0, [ %o2 ]    ! Write length/flags to 0x200028CC
    31d8:	d2 03 00 00 	ld  [ %o4 ], %o1    ! Read controller status 0x200028C0
    31dc:	15 00 00 10 	sethi  %hi(0x4000), %o2
    31e0:	80 8a 40 0a 	btst  %o1, %o2    ! Test busy bit 14
    31e4:	02 80 00 12 	be  0x322c
    31e8:	b0 10 20 00 	clr  %i0
    31ec:	11 00 18 f5 	sethi  %hi(0x63d400), %o0    ! Load polling limit 0x63D76A
    31f0:	a4 12 23 6a 	or  %o0, 0x36a, %l2	! 0x63d76a
    31f4:	a2 10 00 0c 	mov  %o4, %l1
    31f8:	a0 10 00 0a 	mov  %o2, %l0
    31fc:	7f ff fd 4c 	call  0x272c    ! Delay one unit before retry
    3200:	90 10 20 01 	mov  1, %o0
    3204:	b0 06 20 01 	inc  %i0
    3208:	80 a6 00 12 	cmp  %i0, %l2    ! Check timeout counter
    320c:	08 80 00 04 	bleu  0x321c
    3210:	01 00 00 00 	nop 
    3214:	10 80 00 18 	b  0x3274
    3218:	b0 10 3f ff 	mov  -1, %i0	! 0xffffffff    ! Timeout result = -1 (delay slot)
    321c:	d0 04 40 00 	ld  [ %l1 ], %o0
    3220:	80 8a 00 10 	btst  %o0, %l0    ! Test busy bit again
    3224:	12 bf ff f6 	bne  0x31fc
    3228:	01 00 00 00 	nop 
    322c:	11 0c 00 0f 	sethi  %hi(0x30003c00), %o0
    3230:	d2 0a 20 7c 	ldub  [ %o0 + 0x7c ], %o1	! 0x30003c7c    ! Read option byte at 0x30003C7C
    3234:	80 a2 60 00 	cmp  %o1, 0
    3238:	02 80 00 0e 	be  0x3270
    323c:	b0 10 20 00 	clr  %i0
    3240:	80 a6 00 1a 	cmp  %i0, %i2    ! Check word offset against requested size
    3244:	1a 80 00 0c 	bcc  0x3274
    3248:	11 30 7f 35 	sethi  %hi(0xc1fcd400), %o0
    324c:	94 12 20 08 	or  %o0, 8, %o2	! 0xc1fcd408    ! XOR seed 0xC1FCD408
    3250:	d0 06 40 18 	ld  [ %i1 + %i0 ], %o0    ! Load 32-bit word from buffer
    3254:	92 06 00 0a 	add  %i0, %o2, %o1
    3258:	90 1a 00 09 	xor  %o0, %o1, %o0    ! XOR word with seed + offset
    325c:	d0 26 40 18 	st  %o0, [ %i1 + %i0 ]    ! Write transformed word in place
    3260:	b0 06 20 04 	add  %i0, 4, %i0    ! Advance four bytes
    3264:	80 a6 00 1a 	cmp  %i0, %i2
    3268:	2a bf ff fb 	bcs,a   0x3254    ! Annulled branch; next word loaded only on loop
    326c:	d0 06 40 18 	ld  [ %i1 + %i0 ], %o0
    3270:	b0 10 20 00 	clr  %i0    ! Return success = 0
    3274:	81 c7 e0 08 	ret 
    3278:	81 e8 00 00 	restore 

! 0x327C: Hardware status/diagnostic routine
! Program registers, inspect status fields and call diagnostic handler.
    327c:	9d e3 bf 98 	save  %sp, -104, %sp
    3280:	21 08 00 00 	sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %l0
    3284:	96 14 20 40 	or  %l0, 0x40, %o3	! 0x20000040
    3288:	11 00 00 20 	sethi  %hi(0x8000), %o0
    328c:	d0 22 c0 00 	st  %o0, [ %o3 ]    ! Set MMIO 0x20000040 to 0x8000
    3290:	92 14 20 44 	or  %l0, 0x44, %o1
    3294:	90 10 22 62 	mov  0x262, %o0
    3298:	d0 22 40 00 	st  %o0, [ %o1 ]    ! Write 0x262 to 0x20000044
    329c:	94 14 20 50 	or  %l0, 0x50, %o2
    32a0:	11 00 00 6a 	sethi  %hi(0x1a800), %o0
    32a4:	d0 22 80 00 	st  %o0, [ %o2 ]    ! Write 0x1A800 to 0x20000050
    32a8:	92 10 21 00 	mov  0x100, %o1
    32ac:	d2 22 c0 00 	st  %o1, [ %o3 ]    ! Write 0x100 to 0x20000040
    32b0:	7f ff fd 1f 	call  0x272c    ! Wait using delay routine
    32b4:	90 10 23 e8 	mov  0x3e8, %o0
    32b8:	92 14 20 c0 	or  %l0, 0xc0, %o1
    32bc:	d0 02 40 00 	ld  [ %o1 ], %o0    ! Read 0x200000C0
    32c0:	80 8a 20 80 	btst  0x80, %o0    ! Check status bit 7
    32c4:	02 80 00 04 	be  0x32d4
    32c8:	01 00 00 00 	nop 
    32cc:	10 80 00 39 	b  0x33b0
    32d0:	b0 10 20 00 	clr  %i0	! 0x0
    32d4:	d0 02 40 00 	ld  [ %o1 ], %o0
    32d8:	80 8a 20 80 	btst  0x80, %o0
    32dc:	12 80 00 33 	bne  0x33a8
    32e0:	90 10 20 00 	clr  %o0
    32e4:	94 14 20 4c 	or  %l0, 0x4c, %o2
    32e8:	d2 02 80 00 	ld  [ %o2 ], %o1    ! Read status at 0x2000004C
    32ec:	11 00 3f fe 	sethi  %hi(0xfff800), %o0
    32f0:	80 8a 40 08 	btst  %o1, %o0    ! Test upper status bits (mask 0xFFF800)
    32f4:	02 80 00 2d 	be  0x33a8
    32f8:	90 10 20 00 	clr  %o0
    32fc:	a2 10 00 10 	mov  %l0, %l1
    3300:	a0 10 00 0a 	mov  %o2, %l0
    3304:	d0 04 00 00 	ld  [ %l0 ], %o0
    3308:	92 10 20 35 	mov  0x35, %o1
    330c:	91 32 20 0b 	srl  %o0, 0xb, %o0    ! Shift status field down 11 bits
    3310:	b0 22 40 08 	sub  %o1, %o0, %i0    ! Compute diagnostic value 0x35 - field
    3314:	80 a6 20 33 	cmp  %i0, 0x33
    3318:	34 80 00 10 	bg,a   0x3358
    331c:	90 10 20 00 	clr  %o0
    3320:	80 a6 20 31 	cmp  %i0, 0x31
    3324:	14 80 00 08 	bg  0x3344
    3328:	90 10 20 00 	clr  %o0
    332c:	80 a6 20 1f 	cmp  %i0, 0x1f
    3330:	14 80 00 0a 	bg  0x3358
    3334:	01 00 00 00 	nop 
    3338:	80 a6 20 1d 	cmp  %i0, 0x1d
    333c:	04 80 00 05 	ble  0x3350
    3340:	80 a6 20 06 	cmp  %i0, 6
    3344:	40 00 00 d2 	call  0x368c    ! Report diagnostic code 0xF
    3348:	92 10 20 0f 	mov  0xf, %o1
    334c:	30 80 00 0b 	b,a   0x3378
    3350:	04 80 00 05 	ble  0x3364
    3354:	90 10 20 00 	clr  %o0
    3358:	40 00 00 cd 	call  0x368c    ! Report diagnostic code 0
    335c:	92 10 20 00 	clr  %o1
    3360:	30 80 00 06 	b,a   0x3378
    3364:	80 a6 20 04 	cmp  %i0, 4
    3368:	04 80 00 04 	ble  0x3378
    336c:	01 00 00 00 	nop 
    3370:	40 00 00 c7 	call  0x368c    ! Report diagnostic code 0xF
    3374:	92 10 20 0f 	mov  0xf, %o1	! 0xf
    3378:	7f ff fc ed 	call  0x272c
    337c:	90 10 20 01 	mov  1, %o0
    3380:	90 14 60 c0 	or  %l1, 0xc0, %o0    ! Re-read status register 0x200000C0
    3384:	d2 02 00 00 	ld  [ %o0 ], %o1
    3388:	80 8a 60 80 	btst  0x80, %o1
    338c:	12 80 00 07 	bne  0x33a8
    3390:	90 10 20 00 	clr  %o0
    3394:	d2 04 00 00 	ld  [ %l0 ], %o1    ! Re-read status register 0x2000004C
    3398:	11 00 3f fe 	sethi  %hi(0xfff800), %o0
    339c:	80 8a 40 08 	btst  %o1, %o0
    33a0:	12 bf ff d9 	bne  0x3304    ! Repeat while masked status remains set
    33a4:	90 10 20 00 	clr  %o0
    33a8:	40 00 00 b9 	call  0x368c    ! Final diagnostic call
    33ac:	92 10 20 00 	clr  %o1
    33b0:	81 c7 e0 08 	ret 
    33b4:	81 e8 00 00 	restore 

! 0x33B8: 512-byte block validation
! Check trailing marker and calculate a 16-bit word sum against 0xBEEF.
    33b8:	96 10 00 08 	mov  %o0, %o3
    33bc:	c4 0a e1 fe 	ldub  [ %o3 + 0x1fe ], %g2    ! Read byte at block offset 0x1FE
    33c0:	80 a0 a0 30 	cmp  %g2, 0x30    ! Require ASCII 0 (0x30)
    33c4:	12 80 00 06 	bne  0x33dc
    33c8:	94 10 20 00 	clr  %o2
    33cc:	c4 0a e1 ff 	ldub  [ %o3 + 0x1ff ], %g2    ! Read byte at block offset 0x1FF
    33d0:	80 a0 a0 65 	cmp  %g2, 0x65    ! Require ASCII e (0x65)
    33d4:	02 80 00 04 	be  0x33e4
    33d8:	92 10 21 00 	mov  0x100, %o1
    33dc:	10 80 00 13 	b  0x3428
    33e0:	90 10 20 01 	mov  1, %o0    ! Invalid marker -> return 1
    33e4:	85 2a 60 10 	sll  %o1, 0x10, %g2
    33e8:	85 30 a0 10 	srl  %g2, 0x10, %g2
    33ec:	92 02 60 02 	add  %o1, 2, %o1
    33f0:	d0 12 c0 02 	lduh  [ %o3 + %g2 ], %o0    ! Read unsigned 16-bit word from block
    33f4:	87 2a 60 10 	sll  %o1, 0x10, %g3
    33f8:	84 02 80 08 	add  %o2, %o0, %g2    ! Accumulate 16-bit words
    33fc:	87 30 e0 10 	srl  %g3, 0x10, %g3
    3400:	80 a0 e1 ff 	cmp  %g3, 0x1ff    ! Continue through offset 0x1FE
    3404:	08 bf ff f8 	bleu  0x33e4
    3408:	94 10 00 02 	mov  %g2, %o2    ! Update sum in branch delay slot
    340c:	87 28 a0 10 	sll  %g2, 0x10, %g3
    3410:	05 00 00 2f 	sethi  %hi(0xbc00), %g2
    3414:	87 30 e0 10 	srl  %g3, 0x10, %g3
    3418:	84 10 a2 ef 	or  %g2, 0x2ef, %g2    ! Expected 16-bit sum = 0xBEEF

! 0xC000: Boot entry, trap-vector stubs and UART output
! Steps:
! 1. Branch over reserved trap vectors to the startup sequence.
! 2. Provide window overflow/underflow branches and UART transmit helper.

c000: 10 80 00 29    b  0xc0a4                           ! Skip trap-vector area; startup begins at 0xC0A4
c004: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c008: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c00c: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c010: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c014: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c018: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c01c: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c020: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c024: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c028: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c02c: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c030: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c034: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c038: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c03c: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c040: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c044: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c048: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c04c: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c050: 10 80 0e 83    b  0xfa5c                           ! Window overflow trap branch
c054: a1 50 00 00    rd  %wim, %l0                       ! Read window invalid mask in delay slot
c058: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c05c: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c060: 10 80 0e 91    b  0xfaa4                           ! Window underflow trap branch
c064: a1 50 00 00    rd  %wim, %l0                       ! Read WIM in delay slot
c068: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
c06c: 01 00 00 00    nop

ca8c: 96 12 a0 40    or  %o2, 0x40, %o3                  ! 0x20000040
ca90: e4 22 c0 00    st  %l2, [ %o3 ]                    ! Set interface MMIO 0x20000040
ca94: 92 12 a0 44    or  %o2, 0x44, %o1                 
ca98: 90 10 22 62    mov  0x262, %o0                    
ca9c: d0 22 40 00    st  %o0, [ %o1 ]                   
caa0: b0 12 a0 4c    or  %o2, 0x4c, %i0                  ! Write MMIO 0x20000044
caa4: 94 12 a0 50    or  %o2, 0x50, %o2                 
caa8: 11 00 00 10    sethi  %hi(0x4000), %o0            
caac: d0 22 80 00    st  %o0, [ %o2 ]                   
cab0: 92 10 21 00    mov  0x100, %o1                    
cab4: d2 22 c0 00    st  %o1, [ %o3 ]                    ! Enable interface
cab8: 21 00 3f fe    sethi  %hi(0xfff800), %l0          
cabc: 6a 1f 3e 51    call  0xa87dc400                   
cac0: 90 10 23 e8    mov  0x3e8, %o0                    
cac4: 90 10 00 11    mov  %l1, %o0                      
cac8: 7f ff fe 7b    call  0xc4b4                       
cacc: 92 10 20 01    mov  1, %o1                         ! Read device status
cad0: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0                       
cad4: 32 bf ff dd    bne,a   0xca48                     
cad8: 13 21 e0 dc    sethi  %hi(0x87837000), %o1        
cadc: d0 06 00 00    ld  [ %i0 ], %o0                   
cae0: 80 8a 00 10    btst  %o0, %l0                      ! Poll interface status bits
cae4: 12 bf ff f6    bne  0xcabc                        
cae8: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
caec: 80 a4 e1 41    cmp  %l3, 0x141                     ! Branch by detected device ID
caf0: 12 80 00 27    bne  0xcb8c                        
caf4: 92 10 20 1c    mov  0x1c, %o1                     
caf8: 92 10 20 03    mov  3, %o1                        
cafc: 7f ff fe 6e    call  0xc4b4                       
cb00: 90 10 00 11    mov  %l1, %o0                      
cb04: 91 3a 20 04    sra  %o0, 4, %o0                   
cb08: 90 0a 20 3f    and  %o0, 0x3f, %o0                
cb0c: 13 21 e0 dc    sethi  %hi(0x87837000), %o1        
cb10: 80 a2 20 28    cmp  %o0, 0x28                     
cb14: 12 80 00 12    bne  0xcb5c                         ! Check extracted device field 0x28
cb18: d0 32 60 00    sth  %o0, [ %o1 ]                   ! Save field to 0x87837000
cb1c: 92 10 20 16    mov  0x16, %o1                     
cb20: 94 10 20 03    mov  3, %o2                        
cb24: 7f ff fe b0    call  0xc5e4                        ! Program device register 0x16
cb28: 90 10 00 11    mov  %l1, %o0                      
cb2c: 92 10 20 11    mov  0x11, %o1                     
cb30: 94 10 20 10    mov  0x10, %o2                     
cb34: 7f ff fe ac    call  0xc5e4                        ! Program device register 0x11
cb38: 90 10 00 11    mov  %l1, %o0                      
cb3c: 92 10 20 10    mov  0x10, %o1                     
cb40: 94 10 21 77    mov  0x177, %o2                    
cb44: 7f ff fe a8    call  0xc5e4                        ! Program device register 0x10
cb48: 90 10 00 11    mov  %l1, %o0                      
cb4c: 90 10 00 11    mov  %l1, %o0                      
cb50: 92 10 20 16    mov  0x16, %o1                     
cb54: 7f ff fe a4    call  0xc5e4                        ! Program device register 0x16
cb58: 94 10 20 00    clr  %o2                           
cb5c: 92 10 20 11    mov  0x11, %o1                     
cb60: 7f ff fe 55    call  0xc4b4                       
cb64: 90 10 00 11    mov  %l1, %o0                      
cb68: 92 10 00 08    mov  %o0, %o1                      
cb6c: 80 8a 64 00    btst  0x400, %o1                    ! Test device status bit 10
cb70: 02 80 00 04    be  0xcb80                         
cb74: 11 00 00 20    sethi  %hi(0x8000), %o0            
cb78: 10 80 00 03    b  0xcb84                          
cb7c: 90 0a 40 08    and  %o1, %o0, %o0                 
cb80: 90 10 20 00    clr  %o0                           
cb84: 10 80 00 05    b  0xcb98                          
cb88: 92 10 00 08    mov  %o0, %o1                      
cb8c: 7f ff fe 4a    call  0xc4b4                       
cb90: 90 10 00 11    mov  %l1, %o0                      
cb94: 92 0a 20 10    and  %o0, 0x10, %o1                
cb98: 80 a2 60 00    cmp  %o1, 0                         ! Select final configuration based on status
cb9c: 02 80 00 10    be  0xcbdc                         
cba0: 15 08 00 41    sethi  %hi(0x20010400), %o2        
cba4: 11 00 00 3c    sethi  %hi(0xf000), %o0            
cba8: 96 12 a0 1c    or  %o2, 0x1c, %o3                 
cbac: 90 12 22 05    or  %o0, 0x205, %o0                
cbb0: 13 00 01 00    sethi  %hi(0x40000), %o1           
cbb4: d0 22 c0 00    st  %o0, [ %o3 ]                    ! Write final value 0xF205
cbb8: 94 12 a0 18    or  %o2, 0x18, %o2                 
cbbc: 92 12 60 01    or  %o1, 1, %o1                    
cbc0: 96 10 00 0a    mov  %o2, %o3                      
cbc4: d2 22 80 00    st  %o1, [ %o2 ]                   
cbc8: d0 02 c0 00    ld  [ %o3 ], %o0                   
cbcc: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0                       
cbd0: 02 bf ff fe    be  0xcbc8                         
cbd4: b0 10 20 00    clr  %i0                           
cbd8: 30 80 00 0e    b,a   0xcc10                       
cbdc: 11 00 00 3c    sethi  %hi(0xf000), %o0            
cbe0: 96 12 a0 1c    or  %o2, 0x1c, %o3                 
cbe4: 90 12 21 05    or  %o0, 0x105, %o0                 ! Write final value 0xF105
cbe8: 13 00 01 00    sethi  %hi(0x40000), %o1           
cbec: d0 22 c0 00    st  %o0, [ %o3 ]                   
cbf0: 94 12 a0 18    or  %o2, 0x18, %o2                 
cbf4: 92 12 60 01    or  %o1, 1, %o1                    
cbf8: 96 10 00 0a    mov  %o2, %o3                      
cbfc: d2 22 80 00    st  %o1, [ %o2 ]                   
cc00: d0 02 c0 00    ld  [ %o3 ], %o0                   
cc04: 80 8a 20 04    btst  4, %o0                       
cc08: 02 bf ff fe    be  0xcc00                         
cc0c: b0 10 20 00    clr  %i0                           
cc10: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                                 ! Return status
cc14: 81 e8 00 00    restore                            

! 0xCC18: Wait for MMIO command completion
! Steps:
! 1. Poll 0x200000E0 bit 0 with bounded counter.
! 2. Invoke status/error routine 0xFAF4 on timeout.

cc18: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp                ! Entry: wait for command completion
cc1c: 11 08 00 00    sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %o0        
cc20: 94 12 20 e0    or  %o0, 0xe0, %o2                  ! 0x200000e0
cc24: d2 02 80 00    ld  [ %o2 ], %o1                    ! Read status at 0x200000E0
cc28: 11 00 01 24    sethi  %hi(0x49000), %o0           
cc2c: 80 8a 60 01    btst  1, %o1                        ! Test busy bit 0
cc30: 02 80 00 0c    be  0xcc60                         
cc34: a0 12 23 e0    or  %o0, 0x3e0, %l0                
cc38: a2 10 00 0a    mov  %o2, %l1                      
cc3c: a0 84 3f ff    addcc  %l0, -1, %l0                
cc40: 12 80 00 04    bne  0xcc50                         ! Countdown with timeout branch
cc44: 90 10 20 01    mov  1, %o0                        
cc48: 40 00 0b ab    call  0xfaf4                        ! Call status/error handler 0xFAF4
cc4c: 92 10 20 0c    mov  0xc, %o1                      
cc50: d0 04 40 00    ld  [ %l1 ], %o0                   
cc54: 80 8a 20 01    btst  1, %o0                       
cc58: 12 bf ff fa    bne  0xcc40                        
cc5c: a0 84 3f ff    addcc  %l0, -1, %l0                
cc60: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                                
cc64: 81 e8 00 00    restore                            

! 0xCC68: Write two controller data words
! Steps:
! 1. Pack channel and transfer count, write D8/DC and command D0.
! 2. Wait using 0xCC18.

cc68: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp                ! Entry: write controller data words
cc6c: 13 08 00 00    sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %o1        
cc70: 90 12 60 d8    or  %o1, 0xd8, %o0                  ! 0x200000d8
cc74: b6 06 ff ff    add  %i3, -1, %i3                  
cc78: f2 22 00 00    st  %i1, [ %o0 ]                    ! Write first word to 0x200000D8
cc7c: b1 2e 20 01    sll  %i0, 1, %i0                   
cc80: b7 2e e0 08    sll  %i3, 8, %i3                   
cc84: 15 00 00 0a    sethi  %hi(0x2800), %o2            
cc88: 90 12 60 dc    or  %o1, 0xdc, %o0                 
cc8c: b6 16 c0 0a    or  %i3, %o2, %i3                  
cc90: b0 0e 20 fe    and  %i0, 0xfe, %i0                
cc94: b0 16 00 1b    or  %i0, %i3, %i0                  
cc98: f4 22 00 00    st  %i2, [ %o0 ]                    ! Write second word to 0x200000DC
cc9c: 92 12 60 d0    or  %o1, 0xd0, %o1                 
cca0: f0 22 40 00    st  %i0, [ %o1 ]                    ! Issue packed write command
cca4: 7f ff ff dd    call  0xcc18                        ! Wait for completion
cca8: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ccac: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                                
ccb0: 81 e8 00 00    restore                            

! 0xCCB4: Read two controller data words
! Steps:
! 1. Issue read command and wait.
! 2. Copy words from D8/DC to caller pointers.

ccb4: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp                ! Entry: read controller data words
ccb8: b6 06 ff ff    add  %i3, -1, %i3                  
ccbc: b7 2e e0 08    sll  %i3, 8, %i3                   
ccc0: 11 00 00 0a    sethi  %hi(0x2800), %o0            
ccc4: b1 2e 20 01    sll  %i0, 1, %i0                   
ccc8: b6 16 c0 08    or  %i3, %o0, %i3                  
cccc: b0 0e 20 fe    and  %i0, 0xfe, %i0                
ccd0: 21 08 00 00    sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %l0        
ccd4: b0 16 00 1b    or  %i0, %i3, %i0                  
ccd8: 90 14 20 d0    or  %l0, 0xd0, %o0                 
ccdc: b0 16 20 01    or  %i0, 1, %i0                    
cce0: f0 22 00 00    st  %i0, [ %o0 ]                    ! Issue packed read command
cce4: 7f ff ff cd    call  0xcc18                        ! Wait for completion
cce8: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ccec: 90 14 20 d8    or  %l0, 0xd8, %o0                 
ccf0: d2 02 00 00    ld  [ %o0 ], %o1                    ! Read first result word
ccf4: a0 14 20 dc    or  %l0, 0xdc, %l0                 
ccf8: d2 26 40 00    st  %o1, [ %i1 ]                    ! Store first word to caller pointer
ccfc: d0 04 00 00    ld  [ %l0 ], %o0                    ! Read second result word
cd00: d0 26 80 00    st  %o0, [ %i2 ]                    ! Store second word to caller pointer
cd04: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                                
cd08: 81 e8 00 00    restore                            

! 0xCD0C: Read controller status bit
! Steps:
! 1. Issue command, wait and return Boolean derived from E0 bit 1.

cd0c: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp                ! Entry: read status bit
cd10: 11 00 00 0a    sethi  %hi(0x2800), %o0            
cd14: 90 12 20 01    or  %o0, 1, %o0                     ! 0x2801
cd18: b1 2e 20 01    sll  %i0, 1, %i0                   
cd1c: 21 08 00 00    sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %l0        
cd20: b0 16 00 08    or  %i0, %o0, %i0                  
cd24: 92 14 20 d0    or  %l0, 0xd0, %o1                 
cd28: f0 22 40 00    st  %i0, [ %o1 ]                    ! Issue packed status command
cd2c: 7f ff ff bb    call  0xcc18                       
cd30: a0 14 20 e0    or  %l0, 0xe0, %l0                 
cd34: d0 04 00 00    ld  [ %l0 ], %o0                    ! Read status register
cd38: 90 0a 20 02    and  %o0, 2, %o0                    ! Mask status bit 1
cd3c: 80 a0 00 08    cmp  %g0, %o0                      
cd40: b0 60 3f ff    subx  %g0, -1, %i0                  ! Convert status flag to Boolean using carry
cd44: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                                
cd48: 81 e8 00 00    restore                            

! 0xCD4C: Read one device byte
! Steps:
! 1. Write packed request and return selected byte from D8.

cd4c: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp                ! Entry: read device byte
cd50: 13 08 00 00    sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %o1        
cd54: a0 12 60 d8    or  %o1, 0xd8, %l0                  ! 0x200000d8
cd58: b1 2e 20 18    sll  %i0, 0x18, %i0                
cd5c: 11 00 00 2a    sethi  %hi(0xa800), %o0            
cd60: f0 24 00 00    st  %i0, [ %l0 ]                    ! Write index in high byte
cd64: 92 12 60 d0    or  %o1, 0xd0, %o1                 
cd68: 90 12 21 d2    or  %o0, 0x1d2, %o0                
cd6c: d0 22 40 00    st  %o0, [ %o1 ]                    ! Issue command 0xA9D2
cd70: 7f ff ff aa    call  0xcc18                       
cd74: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
cd78: f0 04 00 00    ld  [ %l0 ], %i0                    ! Read result word
cd7c: b1 36 20 10    srl  %i0, 0x10, %i0                
cd80: b0 0e 20 ff    and  %i0, 0xff, %i0                 ! Extract byte
cd84: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                                
cd88: 81 e8 00 00    restore                            

! 0xCD8C: Write one device byte
! Steps:
! 1. Pack index/value and issue command through D0/D8.

cd8c: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp                ! Entry: write device byte
cd90: b2 0e 60 ff    and  %i1, 0xff, %i1                
cd94: 11 00 00 40    sethi  %hi(0x10000), %o0           
cd98: b3 2e 60 08    sll  %i1, 8, %i1                   
cd9c: b2 16 40 08    or  %i1, %o0, %i1                  
cda0: 15 08 00 00    sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %o2        
cda4: b1 2e 20 18    sll  %i0, 0x18, %i0                
cda8: 90 12 a0 d8    or  %o2, 0xd8, %o0                 
cdac: b0 16 00 19    or  %i0, %i1, %i0                  
cdb0: 13 00 00 0a    sethi  %hi(0x2800), %o1            
cdb4: f0 22 00 00    st  %i0, [ %o0 ]                    ! Write packed index and byte
cdb8: 94 12 a0 d0    or  %o2, 0xd0, %o2                 
cdbc: 92 12 63 d2    or  %o1, 0x3d2, %o1                
cdc0: d2 22 80 00    st  %o1, [ %o2 ]                    ! Issue command 0x2BD2
cdc4: 7f ff ff 95    call  0xcc18                       
cdc8: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
cdcc: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                                
cdd0: 81 e8 00 00    restore                            

! 0xCDD4: Signed division helper
! Steps:
! 1. Set Y sign extension, divide, adjust overflow result.

cdd4: 95 3a 20 1f    sra  %o0, 0x1f, %o2                 ! Sign-extend dividend into Y
cdd8: 81 82 a0 00    wr  %o2, %y                        
cddc: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
cde0: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
cde4: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
cde8: 90 fa 00 09    sdivcc  %o0, %o1, %o0               ! Signed divide
cdec: 2e 80 00 02    bvs,a   0xcdf4                     
cdf0: 90 3a 00 00    xnor  %o0, %g0, %o0                
cdf4: 81 c3 e0 08    retl                               
cdf8: 01 00 00 00    nop                                

! 0xCDFC: Unsigned division helper
! Steps:
! 1. Clear Y and divide.

cdfc: 81 80 20 00    wr  %g0, %y                         ! Clear Y for unsigned divide
ce00: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce04: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce08: 81 c3 e0 08    retl                               
ce0c: 90 72 00 09    udiv  %o0, %o1, %o0                 ! Unsigned divide in return delay slot

! 0xCE10: Signed multiplication helper
! Steps:
! 1. Multiply and return low/high halves in o0/o1.

ce10: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce14: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce18: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce1c: 90 5a 00 09    smul  %o0, %o1, %o0                 ! Signed multiply
ce20: 81 c3 e0 08    retl                               
ce24: 93 40 00 00    rd  %y, %o1                         ! Return high product word in delay slot

! 0xCE28: Unsigned multiplication helper
! Steps:
! 1. Multiply and return low/high halves in o0/o1.

ce28: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce2c: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce30: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce34: 90 52 00 09    umul  %o0, %o1, %o0                 ! Unsigned multiply
ce38: 81 c3 e0 08    retl                               
ce3c: 93 40 00 00    rd  %y, %o1                         ! Return high product word in delay slot

! 0xCE40: Signed remainder helper
! Steps:
! 1. Divide, multiply quotient and subtract.

ce40: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce44: 99 3a 20 1f    sra  %o0, 0x1f, %o4                
ce48: 81 83 20 00    wr  %o4, %y                        
ce4c: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce50: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce54: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce58: 94 fa 00 09    sdivcc  %o0, %o1, %o2               ! Signed divide for remainder
ce5c: 2e 80 00 02    bvs,a   0xce64                     
ce60: 94 3a 80 00    xnor  %o2, %g0, %o2                
ce64: 94 5a 80 09    smul  %o2, %o1, %o2                 ! Multiply quotient by divisor
ce68: 81 c3 e0 08    retl                               
ce6c: 90 22 00 0a    sub  %o0, %o2, %o0                  ! Subtract to get remainder in return delay slot

! 0xCE70: Unsigned remainder helper
! Steps:
! 1. Divide, multiply quotient and subtract.

ce70: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce74: 81 80 20 00    wr  %g0, %y                        
ce78: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce7c: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce80: 01 00 00 00    nop                                
ce84: 94 72 00 09    udiv  %o0, %o1, %o2                 ! Unsigned divide for remainder
ce88: 94 52 80 09    umul  %o2, %o1, %o2                
ce8c: 81 c3 e0 08    retl                               
ce90: 90 22 00 0a    sub  %o0, %o2, %o0                  ! Subtract to get remainder in return delay slot

! 0xCE94: Flash logical-to-physical address translation
! Steps:
! 1. If translation flag is set, scan up to 1024 mapping entries.
! 2. Preserve low five bits while substituting the translated page index.

ce94: 05 21 e0 dc    sethi  %hi(0x87837000), %g2         ! Entry: flash address translation
ce98: c6 00 a0 c8    ld  [ %g2 + 0xc8 ], %g3             ! Read translation-enabled flag at 0x878370C8
ce9c: 80 a0 e0 00    cmp  %g3, 0                        
cea0: 02 80 00 16    be  0xcef8                         
cea4: 99 3a 20 05    sra  %o0, 5, %o4                    ! Original address divided by 32
cea8: 92 10 20 00    clr  %o1                           
ceac: 17 21 e0 dc    sethi  %hi(0x87837000), %o3        
ceb0: 80 a2 63 ff    cmp  %o1, 0x3ff                     ! Scan up to 1024 entries
ceb4: 14 80 00 0b    bg  0xcee0                         
ceb8: 95 2a 60 02    sll  %o1, 2, %o2                   
cebc: c6 02 e0 04    ld  [ %o3 + 4 ], %g3               
cec0: 05 00 00 3f    sethi  %hi(0xfc00), %g2            
cec4: c6 10 c0 0a    lduh  [ %g3 + %o2 ], %g3            ! Read 16-bit source page index
cec8: 84 10 a3 ff    or  %g2, 0x3ff, %g2                
cecc: 80 a0 c0 02    cmp  %g3, %g2                      
ced0: 02 80 00 0a    be  0xcef8                          ! Stop if 0xFFFF sentinel
ced4: 80 a0 c0 0c    cmp  %g3, %o4                      
ced8: 32 bf ff f6    bne,a   0xceb0                     
cedc: 92 02 60 01    inc  %o1                           
cee0: c4 02 e0 04    ld  [ %o3 + 4 ], %g2                ! Read translation-table pointer
cee4: 86 0a 20 1f    and  %o0, 0x1f, %g3                
cee8: 84 00 80 0a    add  %g2, %o2, %g2                 
ceec: d0 10 a0 02    lduh  [ %g2 + 2 ], %o0              ! Read translated 16-bit page index
cef0: 91 2a 20 05    sll  %o0, 5, %o0                    ! Multiply translated page index by 32
cef4: 90 12 00 03    or  %o0, %g3, %o0                   ! Preserve original low five bits
cef8: 81 c3 e0 08    retl                               
cefc: 01 00 00 00    nop                                

! 0xCF00: Flash address command
! Steps:
! 1. Translate address and emit command plus three address bytes to MMIO.

cf00: 9d e3 bf 98    save  %sp, -104, %sp                ! Entry: issue flash command/address
cf04: 7f ff ff e4    call  0xce94                        ! Translate requested address
cf08: 90 10 00 19    mov  %i1, %o0                      
cf0c: 13 02 00 40    sethi  %hi(0x8010000), %o1         
cf10: f0 2a 40 00    stb  %i0, [ %o1 ]                   ! Write command byte to 0x08010000
cf14: 15 02 00 80    sethi  %hi(0x8020000), %o2         
cf18: c0 2a 80 00    clrb  [ %o2 ]                      
cf1c: d0 2a 80 00    stb  %o0, [ %o2 ]                   ! Write first address byte to 0x08020000
cf20: 93 3a 20 10    sra  %o0, 0x10, %o1                
cf24: 91 3a 20 08    sra  %o0, 8, %o0                   
cf28: d0 2a 80 00    stb  %o0, [ %o2 ]                   ! Write second address byte to same port
cf2c: 92 0a 60 0f    and  %o1, 0xf, %o1                 
cf30: d2 2a 80 00    stb  %o1, [ %o2 ]                   ! Write third address nibble to same port
cf34: 6a 1f 3d 33    call  0xa87dc400                   
cf38: 90 10 20 0c    mov  0xc, %o0                      
cf3c: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                                
cf40: 81 e8 00 00    restore                            

! 0xCF44: Read 512-byte flash page and optional postprocessing
! Steps:
! 1. Enable flash access, set page address and copy 128 words to destination.
! 2. Optionally transform two 256-byte halves through helpers at 0xD4DC and 0xD578.
! 3. Restore original controller register on exit.

cf44: 9d e3 bf 80    save  %sp, -128, %sp                ! Entry: flash page read
cf48: 11 08 00 00    sethi  %hi(0x20000000), %o0        
cf4c: 90 12 21 0c    or  %o0, 0x10c, %o0                 ! 0x2000010c
cf50: e6 02 00 00    ld  [ %o0 ], %l3                    ! Save original control register
cf54: 92 14 e4 00    or  %l3, 0x400, %o1                
cf58: d2 22 00 00    st  %o1, [ %o0 ]                    ! Temporarily set control bit 10
cf5c: 92 10 00 18    mov  %i0, %o1                      
cf60: 7f ff ff e8    call  0xcf00                        ! Issue flash read command at requested address
cf64: 90 10 20 00    clr  %o0                           
cf68: 13 02 00 00    sethi  %hi(0x8000000), %o1         
cf6c: 94 10 20 00    clr  %o2                           
cf70: d0 02 40 00    ld  [ %o1 ], %o0                    ! Read 32-bit word from flash data window

d020: 81 c7 e0 08    ret                                 ! Return; function ends here
    d024:	81 e8 00 00 	restore 
    d028:	92 10 00 08 	mov  %o0, %o1  ! Start decimal conversion; no digit-range validation is visible.
    d02c:	c4 4a 40 00 	ldsb  [ %o1 ], %g2

    d034:	80 a0 a0 00 	cmp  %g2, 0

    d074:	90 10 00 03 	mov  %g3, %o0


! 0xD078: Colon-delimited firmware metadata parser
! Steps:
! 1. Split an ASCII record into fields using colon delimiters.
! 2. Convert numeric fields and write the results through output pointers.

    d078:	9d e3 bf 58 	save  %sp, -168, %sp
    d07c:	a0 10 00 18 	mov  %i0, %l0
    d080:	b0 10 20 00 	clr  %i0
    d084:	d6 0c 00 18 	ldub  [ %l0 + %i0 ], %o3
    d088:	92 10 00 19 	mov  %i1, %o1
    d08c:	94 10 20 00 	clr  %o2

    d094:	02 80 00 0a 	be  0xd0bc

    d09c:	98 10 00 11 	mov  %l1, %o4

    d0a4:	b0 06 20 01 	inc  %i0
    d0a8:	d0 4c 00 18 	ldsb  [ %l0 + %i0 ], %o0
    d0ac:	94 02 a0 01 	inc  %o2
    d0b0:	80 a2 20 3a 	cmp  %o0, 0x3a
    d0b4:	12 bf ff fb 	bne  0xd0a0
    d0b8:	d6 0c 00 18 	ldub  [ %l0 + %i0 ], %o3
    d0bc:	c0 2c 40 0a 	clrb  [ %l1 + %o2 ]
    d0c0:	40 00 05 f4 	call  0xe890  ! Parse first field using helper at 0xE890 (outside this block).
    d0c4:	90 10 00 11 	mov  %l1, %o0
    d0c8:	80 a2 20 00 	cmp  %o0, 0
    d0cc:	12 80 00 04 	bne  0xd0dc
    d0d0:	b0 06 20 02 	add  %i0, 2, %i0
    d0d4:	10 80 00 2b 	b  0xd180
    d0d8:	c0 26 c0 00 	clr  [ %i3 ]
    d0dc:	d0 4c 00 18 	ldsb  [ %l0 + %i0 ], %o0
    d0e0:	94 10 20 00 	clr  %o2
    d0e4:	80 a2 20 3a 	cmp  %o0, 0x3a
    d0e8:	02 80 00 0a 	be  0xd110
    d0ec:	d2 0c 00 18 	ldub  [ %l0 + %i0 ], %o1

    d0f4:	d2 2a c0 0a 	stb  %o1, [ %o3 + %o2 ]
    d0f8:	b0 06 20 01 	inc  %i0
    d0fc:	d0 4c 00 18 	ldsb  [ %l0 + %i0 ], %o0
    d100:	94 02 a0 01 	inc  %o2
    d104:	80 a2 20 3a 	cmp  %o0, 0x3a

    d10c:	d2 0c 00 18 	ldub  [ %l0 + %i0 ], %o1
    d110:	c0 2c 40 0a 	clrb  [ %l1 + %o2 ]

    d118:	90 10 00 11 	mov  %l1, %o0

! 0xD188: Read flash page-zero metadata marker
! Steps:
! 1. Read NAND page zero into RAM at 0x87800000.
! 2. Return the 32-bit word at page offset 0x1FC.

    d188:	9d e3 bf 98 	save  %sp, -104, %sp  ! Reads flash page 0 into 0x87800000.
    d18c:	21 21 e0 00 	sethi  %hi(0x87800000), %l0
    d190:	90 10 20 00 	clr  %o0
    d194:	7f ff ff 6c 	call  0xcf44
    d198:	92 10 00 10 	mov  %l0, %o1
    d19c:	f0 04 21 fc 	ld  [ %l0 + 0x1fc ], %i0  ! Read marker at byte offset 0x1FC.
    d1a0:	81 c7 e0 08 	ret 
    d1a4:	81 e8 00 00 	restore 


! 0xD1A8: Initialize flash page mapping from DEAD marker
! Steps:
! 1. Reset mapping state and check the 0xDEADxxxx marker.
! 2. Load eight 512-byte pages into the mapping area; return status.

    d1a8:	9d e3 bf 98 	save  %sp, -104, %sp
    d1ac:	13 21 e0 dc 	sethi  %hi(0x87837000), %o1
    d1b0:	11 21 e0 dc 	sethi  %hi(0x87837000), %o0
    d1b4:	15 21 e0 dc 	sethi  %hi(0x87837000), %o2

    d1bc:	c0 32 61 a2 	clrh  [ %o1 + 0x1a2 ]
    d1c0:	c0 2a 21 a0 	clrb  [ %o0 + 0x1a0 ]

    d1c8:	7f ff ff f0 	call  0xd188
    d1cc:	c0 22 e1 a8 	clr  [ %o3 + 0x1a8 ]
    d1d0:	a0 10 00 08 	mov  %o0, %l0
    d1d4:	11 3f ff c0 	sethi  %hi(0xffff0000), %o0  ! Mask upper half of marker for comparison.
    d1d8:	90 0c 00 08 	and  %l0, %o0, %o0
    d1dc:	13 37 ab 40 	sethi  %hi(0xdead0000), %o1
    d1e0:	80 a2 00 09 	cmp  %o0, %o1  ! Check marker prefix 0xDEAD.
    d1e4:	12 80 00 17 	bne  0xd240
    d1e8:	b0 10 20 01 	mov  1, %i0
    d1ec:	11 00 00 3f 	sethi  %hi(0xfc00), %o0
    d1f0:	90 12 23 ff 	or  %o0, 0x3ff, %o0	! 0xffff
    d1f4:	13 21 e0 dc 	sethi  %hi(0x87837000), %o1
    d1f8:	a0 0c 00 08 	and  %l0, %o0, %l0  ! Use low 16 marker bits as page-map count/offset input.
    d1fc:	e2 02 60 04 	ld  [ %o1 + 4 ], %l1
    d200:	a1 2c 20 05 	sll  %l0, 5, %l0  ! Scale marker value by 32.
    d204:	b0 10 20 00 	clr  %i0
    d208:	90 0e 20 ff 	and  %i0, 0xff, %o0
    d20c:	93 2a 20 09 	sll  %o0, 9, %o1
    d210:	90 04 00 08 	add  %l0, %o0, %o0
    d214:	7f ff ff 4c 	call  0xcf44  ! Read 512-byte flash page to mapped RAM destination.
    d218:	92 04 40 09 	add  %l1, %o1, %o1
    d21c:	b0 06 20 01 	inc  %i0
    d220:	90 0e 20 ff 	and  %i0, 0xff, %o0
    d224:	80 a2 20 07 	cmp  %o0, 7  ! Loop over eight pages (0..7).
    d228:	08 bf ff fa 	bleu  0xd210
    d22c:	93 2a 20 09 	sll  %o0, 9, %o1
    d230:	92 10 20 01 	mov  1, %o1
    d234:	11 21 e0 dc 	sethi  %hi(0x87837000), %o0
    d238:	d2 22 20 c8 	st  %o1, [ %o0 + 0xc8 ]	! 0x878370c8  ! Mark page mapping initialized.
    d23c:	b0 10 20 00 	clr  %i0
    d240:	81 c7 e0 08 	ret 
    d244:	81 e8 00 00 	restore 


! 0xD248: Load kernel and initrd from NAND

    d400:	0a bf ff ed 	bcs  0xd3b4

! 0xD4DC: Three-byte diagnostic/check field generator
! Steps:
! 1. Look up each input byte in a RAM table.
! 2. Accumulate bitwise parity/check values and encode a three-byte result.
! 3. A call with unusual encoding at 0xD540 needs independent verification.

    d4dc:	9d e3 bf 98 	save  %sp, -104, %sp
    d4e0:	11 21 e0 da 	sethi  %hi(0x87836800), %o0
    d4e4:	86 12 20 98 	or  %o0, 0x98, %g3	! 0x87836898

    d4ec:	98 10 20 00 	clr  %o4
    d4f0:	9a 10 20 00 	clr  %o5
    d4f4:	c0 2e 60 02 	clrb  [ %i1 + 2 ]
    d4f8:	c0 2e 60 01 	clrb  [ %i1 + 1 ]
    d4fc:	c0 2e 40 00 	clrb  [ %i1 ]
    d500:	96 10 20 00 	clr  %o3
    d504:	d0 0e 00 0b 	ldub  [ %i0 + %o3 ], %o0
    d508:	d4 08 c0 08 	ldub  [ %g3 + %o0 ], %o2  ! Lookup indexed by input byte.
    d50c:	92 0a a0 3f 	and  %o2, 0x3f, %o1
    d510:	a0 1b 40 09 	xor  %o5, %o1, %l0
    d514:	80 8a a0 40 	btst  0x40, %o2  ! Test table flag bit 0x40.
    d518:	02 80 00 04 	be  0xd528
    d51c:	9a 10 00 10 	mov  %l0, %o5
    d520:	84 18 80 0b 	xor  %g2, %o3, %g2
    d524:	98 3b 00 0b 	xnor  %o4, %o3, %o4
    d528:	96 02 e0 01 	inc  %o3
    d52c:	80 a2 e0 ff 	cmp  %o3, 0xff
    d530:	24 bf ff f6 	ble,a   0xd508  ! Process 256 bytes (0 through 255).
    d534:	d0 0e 00 0b 	ldub  [ %i0 + %o3 ], %o0
    d538:	90 0b 20 ff 	and  %o4, 0xff, %o0
    d53c:	92 08 a0 ff 	and  %g2, 0xff, %o1
    d540:	6a 1f 3e 28 	call  0xa87dcde0  ! Suspicious disassembly: call target 0xA87DCDE0; verify instruction encoding and execution context.
    d544:	94 10 00 19 	mov  %i1, %o2
    d548:	d4 0e 40 00 	ldub  [ %i1 ], %o2
    d54c:	90 38 00 10 	xnor  %g0, %l0, %o0
    d550:	d2 0e 60 01 	ldub  [ %i1 + 1 ], %o1
    d554:	91 2a 20 02 	sll  %o0, 2, %o0
    d558:	94 38 00 0a 	xnor  %g0, %o2, %o2
    d55c:	92 38 00 09 	xnor  %g0, %o1, %o1
    d560:	90 12 20 03 	or  %o0, 3, %o0
    d564:	d0 2e 60 02 	stb  %o0, [ %i1 + 2 ]  ! Write three-byte check field.
    d568:	d4 2e 40 00 	stb  %o2, [ %i1 ]
    d56c:	d2 2e 60 01 	stb  %o1, [ %i1 + 1 ]
    d570:	81 c7 e0 08 	ret 
    d574:	81 e8 00 00 	restore
